- **Fix:** Remove or substantiate this hypothesis. If a tab-key mismatch is suspected at the hook's call site (wrong estimateTabId passed in), cite that location; otherwise drop it to avoid a dead-end investigation.

### [MEDIUM] A4-F6 — U5 normalization is sound but under-specified; the server already normalizes, so the real gap is the optimistic clone  _(type: risk)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md:96 (KTD5 at :37)
- **Plan claim:** Add a normalization step on insert for rows with a source id but missing link state.
- **Ground truth:** Server already derives linkState from sourceLibraryItemId (actions.ts:2552, 784); the clone (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352) leaves the optimistic linkState undefined.
- **Detail:** U5 proposes to 'Add an explicit normalization step if library rows arrive without link state but have a source id' (04-implementation-plan.md:96; KTD5 at :37). Ground truth: the server insert ALREADY normalizes via linkState: sourceLibraryItemId ? 'linked-clean' : 'unlinked' (actions.ts:2552), and getSourceLibraryItemId falls back to libraryItem.id when source==='library' and id is a UUID (actions.ts:784). The server is not the gap. The gap, if any, is the OPTIMISTIC client row: cloneLibraryItemForEstimate (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352-369) does NOT set linkState, so the optimistic row relies solely on the sourceLibraryItemId branch of the gate. If the gate were ever tightened to require a linkState, optimistic rows would regress. The plan should pin normalization to the clone function, not 'somewhere on insert'.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:2552, src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:784, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352-369
- **Fix:** Make U5 concrete: in cloneLibraryItemForEstimate, set linkState = sourceLibraryItemId ? 'linked-clean' : existing so optimistic and server rows agree. Add a unit test asserting the cloned optimistic row satisfies canMakeProjectSpecific for a DB-backed component (UUID id + sourceLibraryItemId).

It should derive from row source/link fields', 03-ux-interaction-design.md:76) are both verified correct: canMakeProjectSpecific (cells.tsx:79-82) reads only item.sourceLibraryItemId and item.linkState; there is no rowIndex, duplicate-count, or sibling lookup in the gate or its callers (requestProjectSpecific, EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2068-2072). The plan's framing of the correct fix shape is accurate even though its root-cause mechanism is unproven.

### [MEDIUM] A4-F8 — Missing tests for the actual gate boundary (source id present, linkState undefined) and an order-independence regression test  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md:97; AE3/AE4 at :125-126
- **Plan claim:** U5 tests: two same-library rows both show the Cube action; detaching the first flips it to badge.
- **Ground truth:** The four linkState/sourceLibraryItemId combinations are all reachable; the optimistic 'source id present, linkState undefined' case is unit-untested, and insert uses refetchType:'none' so the optimistic row is what users see.
- **Detail:** U5's tests assert the symptom ('Two same-library component rows both show the Cube action; detaching the first updates it to badge state immediately', 04-implementation-plan.md:97; AE3/AE4 at :125-126) but not the mechanism. The gate's true boundaries — (a) sourceLibraryItemId present + linkState undefined => passes; (b) sourceLibraryItemId null + linkState 'unlinked' => fails; (c) linkState 'source-changed' => passes; (d) linkState 'detached' => badge — are all reachable in canMakeProjectSpecific (cells.tsx:80-81) and case (a), which is exactly the optimistic-insert state, is currently untested at the unit level.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/cells.tsx:80-81, src/lib/estimates/types.ts:2, src/hooks/use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:144
- **Fix:** Add unit tests for canMakeProjectSpecific covering all four combinations, plus an integration test inserting the SAME DB-backed component twice and asserting both optimistic rows pass the gate before any refetch. This directly validates AE3 against the real optimistic path.

## Lane A5-components-tab-projection — readiness 58/100
**Summary:** The plan's factual claims about the Components-tab code are almost entirely accurate: the file/symbol references for LibraryComponentsRoute, LibraryPageClient, ComponentLibraryManager, LibraryFolderTree, library-folder-reorder, and TemplatesTree all resolve to the right symbols (line numbers are off by 0-13 in a few cases but the behavior described is correct). The serious problem is the central feasibility question. The plan's KTD6 ("Project renders only when projectId is supplied") is framed as a guard on existing plumbing, but NO projectId reaches the Components tab today: the route /workshop/library/components is a workspace-only surface, its page.tsx reads searchParams only to discard them, LibraryComponentsRoute takes no props, LibraryPageClient/ComponentLibraryManager have no projectId prop, and getCurrentWorkspaceComponentRowsLibrary() is hard-scoped to the default workspace. The projectId-bearing library surface (component-library/index.tsx EstimateRightSidebar, projectId at :608/:777) is a DIFFERENT component the plan does not target. The plan also understates that injecting a virtual node BEFORE "All Components" collides with hard structural assumptions in the folder-tree builder, reorder placement (nodes[0] synthetic-root math), selection, and drag/drop, which only model two datum kinds (folder/component). A mitigating fact: ComponentLibraryManager already calls useSearchParams(), so a ?projectId= query path is reachable — but the route threading, the project-scoped data action, and the projection are all net-new. Net: claims verify well, but the slice's hardest dependency (project context + a new tree-builder shape) is real net-new work the plan treats as a small projection.

**Dimension scores:** Claim accuracy (current-state references) 88; Feasibility realism (projectId plumbing) 40; Tree/DnD compatibility analysis 45; Data-source completeness 55; Scoping clarity (which surface) 50

**Strengths:**
- Factual code references are high-quality: LibraryComponentsRoute, LibraryPageClient query key, ComponentLibraryManager folder-tree build, LibraryFolderTree synthetic root, library-folder-reorder kinds, and all four TemplatesTree functions resolve to the correct symbols with correct described behavior.
- The plan correctly identifies that project rows should be a projection and must NOT be written into library_folders — this is well-aligned with the actual table being workspace-scoped (LibraryFolderRecord.workspace_id) and the DnD/reorder logic being library_folders-only.
- The exclusion rule citing isBlankEstimateRowItem is grounded: the helper genuinely exists (activity-history.tsx:120) and classifies project/BLANK_LABEL/zero-cost placeholders, so the projection's 'exclude blanks' rule is implementable today.
- KTD6's instinct that the workspace-only Components route must not silently aggregate project rows is correct and matches the real architecture (workspace route vs /workshop/projects/[projectId]/...).
- The data-model doc correctly notes the existing EstimateRowLinkState enum already has detached/unlinked/linked-overridden states (types.ts:2), so no enum migration is needed for the projection's link-state inclusion rules.

**Findings:**

### [CRITICAL] A5-F1 — KTD6 understates that NO projectId reaches the Components tab today — it is net-new plumbing, not a guard  _(type: inaccuracy)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md KTD6 (line 38) and U6 (lines 99-104); 03-ux-interaction-design.md 'Project Folder In Components' (lines 53-55); 02-data-model... 'Project Folder In Components Tab' (lines 86-90)
- **Plan claim:** KTD6/U6: 'The first implementation renders Project only when a projectId is supplied by route/query/context' — phrased as gating existing plumbing.
- **Ground truth:** The Components route is workspace-only and has zero project context. src/app/workshop/library/components/page.tsx:8-11 reads searchParams ONLY for assertRouteErrorSmoke and renders <LibraryComponentsRoute/> with no args. LibraryComponentsRoute.tsx:9 takes no props and calls workspace-scoped getCurrentWorkspaceComponentRowsLibrary(). component-library-repository.ts:792-795 resolves ensureDefaultWorkspace() and queries by workspace_id only. LibraryPageClient (src/components/workshop/LibraryPageClient.tsx:66-73) and ComponentLibraryManager (ComponentLibraryManager.tsx:1062-1083) have no projectId prop. Projects live under a separate route tree /workshop/projects/[projectId]/... and the Library tab nav links (LibraryTabsLayoutClient.tsx:25,31,37) are static hrefs with no projectId.
- **Detail:** The plan treats 'when projectId is supplied' as a conditional branch over existing context, but the project context does not exist on this surface at all. Supplying it requires: (a) a project-scoped Library entrypoint or a ?projectId= query param threaded through page.tsx (currently discarded), (b) propagating projectId through LibraryComponentsRoute -> LibraryPageClient -> ComponentLibraryManager, and (c) a new project-scoped data action. U6's file list omits the route page.tsx entirely. This is the single highest-risk gap in the slice.
- **Evidence:** src/app/workshop/library/components/page.tsx:8, src/components/workshop/library/LibraryComponentsRoute.tsx:9, src/lib/repositories/component-library-repository.ts:792, src/components/workshop/LibraryPageClient.tsx:66, src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/ComponentLibraryManager.tsx:1062, src/components/workshop/library/LibraryTabsLayoutClient.tsx:25
- **Fix:** Reframe KTD6/U6 from 'render only when projectId supplied' to 'introduce project context to the Components surface (new requirement)'. Add src/app/workshop/library/components/page.tsx to U6's file list and specify the exact mechanism: query param (?projectId=) parsed in page.tsx and threaded as a prop, or a new project-scoped route. Add a dedicated 'project context plumbing' implementation step before the projection work.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→critical) — Confirmed with one framing nuance. Ground truth verified exactly: src/app/workshop/library/components/page.tsx:8-11 reads searchParams ONLY for assertRouteErrorSmoke and renders <LibraryComponentsRoute/> with no args; LibraryComponentsRoute.tsx:9 takes no props and calls workspace-scoped getCurrentWorkspaceComponentRowsLibrary(); component-library-repository.ts:792-795 resolves ensureDefaultWorkspace() and queries by workspace.id only; LibraryPageClient (LibraryPageClient.tsx:66-73) and ComponentLibraryManager (ComponentLibraryManager.tsx:1062-1083) have NO projectId prop; the tab nav hrefs (LibraryTabsLayoutClient.tsx:25/31/37) are static strings. There is no projectId anywhere in the page.tsx -> LibraryComponentsRoute -> LibraryPageClient -> ComponentLibraryManager chain (grep returned zero hits). The finding's substantive claim is correct and the gap is real and critical: U6's Files list (04-implementation-plan.md:102) names LibraryPageClient, ComponentLibraryManager, LibraryFolderTree, and a new repo/action but OMITS page.tsx and the route shell, and never accounts for the net-new work of threading a ?projectId query param + propagating it through three layers + a new project-scoped action. KTD6/U6 phrase projectId purely as a guard/precondition ('renders Project only when a projectId is supplied by route/query/context'), not as plumbing to build. ONE CORRECTION to the finding's title framing: it overstates slightly by saying KTD6 'understates that NO projectId reaches the tab' — the plan's OWN audit (01-current-state-audit.md:77) explicitly states 'The Components route loads workspace library components, not project-local rows,' and KTD6 forbids workspace-wide aggregation, so the plan is aware the surface is workspace-only. The accurate critique is narrower: the plan acknowledges the surface is workspace-only but treats projectId as if it could simply 'be supplied' and silently assumes the propagation path exists, omitting page.tsx and the plumbing effort entirely. Severity stays critical: this is the single highest-risk unscoped dependency in the slice.

### [HIGH] A5-F2 — Plan conflates the workspace Components tab (no projectId) with the estimate-side library picker (has projectId)  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U6 (lines 99-104); 03-ux-interaction-design.md lines 49-61
- **Plan claim:** U6 targets ComponentLibraryManager/LibraryFolderTree/LibraryPageClient as 'the Components tab'; UX doc says 'The Components tab should gain a Project folder/view'.
- **Ground truth:** There are two distinct library surfaces. The workspace Components tab = ComponentLibraryManager via LibraryPageClient/LibraryComponentsRoute (NO projectId). The estimate-side picker = EstimateRightSidebar in src/components/workshop/component-library/index.tsx, which IS mounted inside the estimate sheet and DOES have projectId (prop at index.tsx:608, destructured at :777) and already renders project sources via TemplatesTree. EstimateSheetContent.tsx:31 imports EstimateRightSidebar from './component-library'.
- **Detail:** The only library surface where a projectId is genuinely in scope today (the estimate-side picker) is NOT the surface U6 modifies. If the product intent is 'see project components while editing an estimate', the estimate-side picker (which already has projectId AND project-source tree wiring) is the natural home and would sidestep most of A5-F1. If the intent is the standalone workspace tab, the projectId problem is unavoidable. The plan never disambiguates which surface the user means.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/component-library/index.tsx:608, src/components/workshop/component-library/index.tsx:777, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:31, src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/ComponentLibraryManager.tsx:1062
- **Fix:** Explicitly state which surface hosts the Project projection. If it can live in the estimate-side EstimateRightSidebar (component-library/index.tsx), note that projectId already flows there and reconsider whether the workspace-tab plumbing (A5-F1) is even needed for MVP. If it must be the workspace tab, acknowledge the picker as a cheaper alternative and justify the harder path.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Confirmed exactly; all cited lines verified. Two distinct library surfaces exist. (1) The workspace Components tab = ComponentLibraryManager mounted via LibraryPageClient/LibraryComponentsRoute, whose props (ComponentLibraryManager.tsx:1062-1083: categoryTree, suppliers, brands, libraryFolders, libraryItems, onLibraryItemsMutated, onLibraryFoldersMutated) include NO projectId and NO project-source data. (2) The estimate-side picker = EstimateRightSidebar in src/components/workshop/component-library/index.tsx, which DOES declare projectId (prop type at index.tsx:608, destructured in EstimateRightSidebarBase at :777) AND already carries projectSources/onInsertProjectSource (index.tsx:618-619, 787-788) and renders project sources through TemplatesTree (imported at index.tsx:79; project-source tree appended per 01-current-state-audit.md:88-91 citing TemplatesTree.tsx:404/423). EstimateSheetContent.tsx:31 confirms 'import { EstimateRightSidebar } from "./component-library"', so the picker is mounted inside the estimate sheet where projectId is genuinely in scope. U6 targets only surface (1) (ComponentLibraryManager/LibraryPageClient/LibraryFolderTree per 04-implementation-plan.md:102) yet the UX doc (03-ux-interaction-design.md:49,54) phrases the intent as 'the Components tab should gain a Project folder/view' without disambiguating which surface the user means. The plan never resolves whether the user wants the standalone workspace tab (where projectId does not exist and F1's plumbing problem is unavoidable) or the estimate-side picker (which already has projectId + project-source wiring and would sidestep most of F1). High severity is appropriate — this ambiguity directly drives the F1 risk and could mean U6 modifies the wrong component.

### [HIGH] A5-F3 — Injecting a node before 'All Components' breaks folder-tree builder, reorder, selection, and DnD assumptions the plan calls 'compatible'  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U6 (line 103); 02-data-model... line 91; 03-ux-interaction-design.md line 54
- **Plan claim:** U6: 'Add a virtual Project node to folder mode ... Place it before All Components'; plan implies this is a simple projection alongside the existing tree.
- **Ground truth:** The folder tree is built by withAllComponentsRoot (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:189) which returns a SINGLE-root array. buildFolderPlacement (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:282-303) hardcodes 'hasSyntheticRoot = nodes.length>0 && isAllComponentsFolderId(nodes[0].folderId)' (line 297) and computes topLevelCount/childFolderCount from nodes[0]. The UI/DnD layer models only two datum kinds — {kind:'folder', isSyntheticRoot} and {kind:'component'} (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:58, 240, 251). resolveFolderDropPlan (lines 532-551) and handleCanDrop (553-561) branch entirely on folder/synthetic-root/component. LibraryFolderKind is the closed union 'taxonomy'|'custom' (library-folder-reorder.ts:10).
- **Detail:** Putting 'Project' at index 0 would make nodes[0] no longer the synthetic All-Components root, breaking the reorder placement math (topLevelCount, index-by-tree-id) for ALL real folders, not just the Project node. The Project node also needs a third datum kind so canDrop/onDrop treat it as non-droppable/non-draggable (it isn't a library_folders row). None of this is captured as work; U6 reads as 'add a node to the array'.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/LibraryFolderTree.tsx:189, src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/LibraryFolderTree.tsx:297, src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/LibraryFolderTree.tsx:540, src/components/workshop/component-library-manager/LibraryFolderTree.tsx:553, src/lib/component-library/library-folder-reorder.ts:10
- **Fix:** Add explicit sub-tasks to U6: (1) generalize withAllComponentsRoot / buildFolderPlacement so the synthetic-root detection is not positional (don't assume nodes[0]); (2) add a third LibraryFolderTreeDatum kind (e.g. 'project-virtual') that is non-selectable for reorder, returns false from handleCanDrop, and is skipped by resolveFolderDropPlan; (3) confirm component leaves under Project use the existing component datum but with a project-row parent that is NOT a library folder id.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Confirmed; every cited mechanic verified at the correct lines. withAllComponentsRoot (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:189-199) returns a SINGLE-root array whose [0] is the synthetic 'All Components' folder. buildFolderPlacement (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:282-303) hardcodes 'const hasSyntheticRoot = nodes.length > 0 && isAllComponentsFolderId(nodes[0].folderId)' (line 297) and derives topLevelFolders/topLevelCount and childFolderCountByFolderId from nodes[0].children (lines 298-302). The UI/DnD datum is a CLOSED two-kind union — { kind: 'folder'; ...; isSyntheticRoot } | { kind: 'component'; ... } (LibraryFolderTree.tsx:57-59). resolveFolderDropPlan (532-551) branches on datum.kind === 'folder' and datum.isSyntheticRoot (e.g. 538, 540, 546), and handleCanDrop (553-561) branches entirely on component vs folder. LibraryFolderKind is the closed union 'taxonomy' | 'custom' (library-folder-reorder.ts:10). The finding's conclusion holds: placing a virtual 'Project' node at index 0 would make nodes[0] no longer the synthetic All-Components root, so hasSyntheticRoot would mis-detect, breaking topLevelCount/indexByTreeId placement math for ALL real folders (not just Project), and the Project node would need a THIRD datum kind so handleCanDrop/resolveFolderDropPlan treat it as non-droppable/non-draggable (it has no library_folders row and is not in the taxonomy|custom union). None of this third-datum-kind / placement-math impact is captured in U6 (04-implementation-plan.md:103 reads 'Add a virtual Project node ... Place it before All Components' as a simple array insertion); the plan's own audit even cites LibraryFolderTree.tsx:189 and library-folder-reorder.ts:10 (01-current-state-audit.md:83-84) without drawing the breakage conclusion. High severity is correct. Minor note: the alternative of placing Project as a SIBLING root (making nodes[0] still All-Components but adding a second top-level entry) would also break the single-root assumption since topLevelFolders is read from nodes[0].children, not from a multi-root scan — reinforcing the finding rather than weakening it.

### [MEDIUM] A5-F4 — Express rows recommended as link_state='unlinked' are indistinguishable from other non-blank unlinked rows except via net-new metadata.expressComponent  _(type: risk)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md lines 36, 95-98; 04-implementation-plan.md KTD2 (line 34)
- **Plan claim:** 02-data-model: express row uses 'link_state = "unlinked"' and is identified by 'metadata.expressComponent.kind = "express"'; projection 'Include rows that are unlinked but no longer blank placeholders'.
- **Ground truth:** metadata.expressComponent does not exist anywhere in the codebase today (grep returns no matches outside .ai/). isBlankEstimateRowItem (activity-history.tsx:120-134) classifies blanks by source==='project' + BLANK label + zero cost + Unassigned + no image, OR the BLANK tag. The link_state enum (types.ts:2) is 'unlinked'|'linked-clean'|'linked-overridden'|'detached'|'source-changed'.
- **Detail:** The inclusion rules mix three signals: expressComponent metadata (net-new, must be reliably written on insert), link_state==='detached', and 'unlinked but not blank'. The third rule is fragile — a half-edited blank or any future unlinked row could leak into the projection. The projection's correctness depends entirely on the new metadata.expressComponent being written atomically at insert time (U2's job), so A5/U6 has a hard hidden dependency on U2's history/template work.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:120, src/lib/estimates/types.ts:2
- **Fix:** State that the projection's primary inclusion signal is metadata.expressComponent.kind==='express' (not the brittle 'unlinked but not blank' fallback), and call out the U2 dependency explicitly. Define behavior for legacy unlinked-non-blank rows that predate expressComponent metadata so they don't silently appear or vanish.

### [MEDIUM] A5-F5 — No existing project-scoped action returns estimate rows for a Components projection — it is net-new  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U6 (line 103); 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md lines 90, 101
- **Plan claim:** U6/02-data-model: 'Load project-local rows through a dedicated project-scoped action that uses project/estimate access checks'; 'do not reuse a workspace-library membership query'.

The plan itself notes the reusable-source collector would over-show templates/projects (02-data-model line 101). No purpose-built 'project express+detached components' action exists.
- **Detail:** The plan correctly flags this as 'dedicated' work but lists it only as 'new repository/action under src/lib/reusable-sources/ or src/lib/component-library/' without specifying access boundary, input (projectId vs estimateId), or which estimates within a project are aggregated. Combined with A5-F1 (no projectId on the surface) and A5-F4 (depends on new metadata), the data path for U6 is almost entirely greenfield.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/reusable-sources/actions.ts:80, src/lib/reusable-sources/actions.ts:293
- **Fix:** Specify the new action signature: input (projectId, optionally a single estimateId), access check (can_access_estimate parity per row's estimate), output (flat list of express + detached EstimateRowItem with their estimate/node provenance), and whether it spans all estimates in the project or a single active estimate. Decide the aggregation scope before U6.

### [LOW] A5-F7 — library-folder-reorder.ts:10 is a TYPE union, not a runtime guard — a 'Project' kind needs a separate node shape  _(type: inaccuracy)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md line 84

- **Ground truth:** library-folder-reorder.ts:10 is 'export type LibraryFolderKind = "taxonomy" | "custom";' — a compile-time union on LibraryFolderRecord (which carries workspace_id, line 12-20). It constrains what can be persisted/reordered as a library_folders row, not a runtime filter.

- **Evidence:** src/lib/component-library/library-folder-reorder.ts:10, src/lib/component-library/library-folder-reorder.ts:12

## Lane A6-datamodel-rpc-sql — readiness 68/100
**Summary:** The SQL-level claims in the Express Components data-model pack are largely accurate and well-grounded. I confirmed against the actual migrations: all 15 named columns on estimate_row_items exist (added across core + later migrations), the link_state enum's five values are REAL and the plan invents none, the update RPC genuinely replaces (not merges) metadata/source_snapshot, the unlink RPC exists and sets exactly what the plan describes, and RLS uses can_access_estimate/can_edit_estimate for rows and is_workspace_member/is_workspace_editor for the library tables at the SQL level. The plan's headline defect claim is correct: insert_estimate_row_item_with_history classifies source-less inserts as 'row_blank_added' (line 422), so express rows would be mislabeled. However the plan materially UNDER-specifies the fix: it never reconciles the SQL's source_library_item_id-IS-NULL heuristic with the client's isBlankEstimateRowItem (material label + tag), never identifies the concrete discriminator available in the payload (material/metadata.tags), and overlooks that express, detached, and blank rows all share source_library_item_id=null and derive source='project'. The plan's entire metadata.expressComponent identity model also has no representation in the estimate-sheet repository SELECT or the EstimateRowItem type — a concrete unmentioned implementation gap. Several smaller gaps (platform-admin policy arm, area-node constraint trigger, source being derived not stored, event_type being unconstrained text) round out the picture.

**Dimension scores:** SQL claim accuracy (columns, RPCs, enum) 88; RLS / provider evidence fidelity 82; Completeness of the blank/express discrimination fix 55; Repository/type readiness for metadata-driven identity 50; Conversion-path SQL correctness (promotion/detach) 70; Coverage of hidden SQL constraints/mechanics 65

**Strengths:**
- link_state enum values are 100% accurate: the plan uses exactly the five real enum members ('unlinked','linked-clean','linked-overridden','detached','source-changed') defined at supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:10 — none are invented, which is a strong, verifiable consistency win.
- The headline defect is real and precisely located: insert RPC line 422 (supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql) literally does `case when inserted_row.source_library_item_id is null then 'row_blank_added' else 'row_added' end`, exactly as the plan claims at 02-data-model:52.
- The replace-vs-merge claim is correct: update RPC lines 517-518 wholesale-replace source_snapshot and metadata from the patch, validating the plan's insistence (02:116, 05:74) on server-side merge.
- RLS claims hold at the SQL level: estimate_row_items policies use can_access_estimate/can_edit_estimate (20260614144136:90-122); cost_library_items uses is_workspace_member/is_workspace_editor (20260526142913:760-762); library_folders uses the same (20260603235355:192-205).
- The unlink RPC description is faithful: it exists (20260601184343), preserves source_snapshot (line 73), sets link_state='detached' (74), MERGES detach metadata with || (75), and guards that the row was actually linked (60-62) — matching the plan's detach model (02:71-80).
- The cited promotion entrypoint is accurate: src/lib/component-library/actions.ts:431 is indeed createCostLibraryComponent, which creates a cost_library_items component with metadata provenance, as the plan states (02:59).

**Findings:**

### [HIGH] A6-F1 — Plan understates the blank-classification fix: SQL heuristic and client isBlankEstimateRowItem use incompatible definitions of "blank"  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:52 (also 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md:73)
- **Plan claim:** "The current insert RPC classifies source-less rows as `row_blank_added`; the implementation must narrow that branch to legacy blank placeholders only."
- **Ground truth:** The SQL branch keys ONLY on `source_library_item_id is null` (insert RPC line 422). But the actual blank-placeholder definition (client isBlankEstimateRowItem, activity-history.tsx:120-134) requires source='project' AND material === 'Drag or add a component here' (BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL) AND (tag 'manual-row-placeholder' OR a persisted-placeholder field match). The two definitions are not equivalent: the SQL test is far looser and already mislabels EVERY source-less insert (express rows, future detached-then-reinserted rows, any project-authored row) as blank — not just express rows.
- **Detail:** The plan correctly flags that express rows get mislabeled, but frames it as a narrow express-vs-blank issue. The real defect is that the SQL has no access to the client's blank heuristic and the plan never supplies a concrete SQL-level discriminator. The only fields in row_payload that can distinguish a true blank placeholder are material (= the blank label) and metadata.tags (containing 'manual-row-placeholder'). The plan should specify that the narrowed branch test is e.g. `row_payload->>'material' = 'Drag or add a component here' OR (row_payload->'metadata'->'tags') ? 'manual-row-placeholder'` rather than the current source-null test.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:422, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:120-134, src/lib/activity/activity-composer.ts (BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL = "Drag or add a component here"), src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx (BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_TAG = "manual-row-placeholder")
- **Fix:** Replace the vague "narrow to legacy blank placeholders only" with an explicit SQL condition that mirrors isBlankEstimateRowItem: classify as 'row_blank_added' only when the inserted material equals BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL and/or metadata.tags includes 'manual-row-placeholder'. Note that the client and SQL definitions of blank must stay in sync, and call out that constant duplication risk.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Independently re-opened all cited files. The core defect is real and the proposed fix is technically valid. The SQL insert branch keys ONLY on source-null: supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:422 reads `case when inserted_row.source_library_item_id is null then 'row_blank_added' else 'row_added' end` (verified as the sole occurrence of both `row_blank_added` and the `source_library_item_id is null` test in the file). The actual client blank definition is far stricter: activity-history.tsx:120-134 requires source==='project' AND material===BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL AND (tag 'manual-row-placeholder' OR a persisted-placeholder field bundle: itemCost===0, category==='Unassigned', !imageUrl). Label constant confirmed at activity-composer.ts:28 ("Drag or add a component here"); tag constant confirmed at activity-history.tsx:104 (BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_TAG = "manual-row-placeholder"). The two definitions are genuinely incompatible and the plan supplies no concrete SQL-level discriminator (verified: no plan file mentions a material/metadata.tags SQL test; plan only says 'narrow that branch to legacy blank placeholders only' at 02-data-model...md:52 and 04-implementation-plan.md:74). CRUCIALLY, I verified F1's PROPOSED discriminator is actually persistable: getEstimateRowMetadata writes `tags: libraryItem.tags ?? []` into metadata.tags (actions.ts:778), and the blank template sets material=BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL and tags=[BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_TAG] (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:223,236), and the insert RPC persists `coalesce(row_payload->'metadata','{}')` (migration:397). So `row_payload->>'material' = 'Drag or add a component here' OR (row_payload->'metadata'->'tags') ? 'manual-row-placeholder'` IS a valid SQL test against the persisted shape. ONE correction to the finding's wording: it claims the SQL 'already mislabels EVERY source-less insert ... not just express rows' — true blank placeholders are themselves source-less and ARE correctly classified as blank today (just via an over-broad test); what is actually mislabeled is every source-less NON-blank insert (express, future detached-then-reinserted, project-authored). Net defect stands; 'EVERY source-less insert' overstates by one category. Severity high is correct: silently mislabeling express inserts as blank corrupts activity history and any downstream blank-vs-express filtering.

### [HIGH] A6-F2 — Express identity model (metadata.expressComponent) is not exposed by the estimate-sheet repository SELECT or the EstimateRowItem type  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:39-48, 95
- **Plan claim:** Inclusion/exclusion driven by `metadata.expressComponent.kind = "express"` and a stable metadata object {kind, createdFrom, projectScoped, promotedLibraryItemId}.
- **Ground truth:** The estimate-sheet read path does NOT fetch metadata. ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT (estimate-repository.ts:45) omits the metadata column entirely, and toEstimateRowItem (estimate-repository.ts:71-92) maps no metadata. The EstimateRowItem type (src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81-120+) has tags?: string[] but NO metadata field at all. So metadata.expressComponent currently has zero representation in the estimate sheet's data flow.
- **Detail:** The plan's central identity mechanism is metadata.expressComponent, yet the primary repository/type that powers the estimate sheet never selects or surfaces metadata. Implementing the plan requires (a) adding `metadata` to ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT, (b) mapping it in toEstimateRowItem, and (c) extending the EstimateRowItem type. None of this is mentioned. This is a concrete, load-bearing repository change hidden behind the plan's 'no migration required' framing.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/repositories/estimate-repository.ts:45, src/lib/repositories/estimate-repository.ts:71-92, src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81-120
- **Fix:** Add an explicit work item: extend ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT and toEstimateRowItem to fetch/map metadata, and add a metadata (or expressComponent) field to the EstimateRowItem type. State that without this the metadata.expressComponent inclusion rule cannot be evaluated client-side.
- **Adversarial verify:** `understated` (sev→high) — Re-opened the repository and type. Every concrete claim is confirmed, and the gap is actually WIDER than stated, so I mark it understated rather than merely confirmed. Confirmed facts: ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT (estimate-repository.ts:45) lists only id, estimate_id, node_id, sort_order, source_library_item_id, source_snapshot, material, description, supplier, unit, quantity, unit_cost, contingency_percentage, misc_cost, row_revision, discipline, designation, link_state — NO metadata column. toEstimateRowItem (estimate-repository.ts:71-92) maps none of metadata. The two estimate-sheet read paths both use this SELECT+mapper (estimate-repository.ts:168-182 and 206-214 via `.from('estimate_row_items').select(ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT)...map(toEstimateRowItem)`), so the rendered sheet's row objects carry zero metadata. EstimateRowItem type confirmed to have NO metadata field. CORRECTION to the finding's line cite: it cites types.ts:81-120; the type actually spans 81-136, and the `metadata?: unknown` at types.ts:29 belongs to ComponentVariantAttachment, NOT EstimateRowItem — so the finding is right that EstimateRowItem lacks metadata, just slightly off on the span (minor). IMPORTANT NUANCE the finding handles correctly: the SQL estimate_row_item_snapshot function DOES expose metadata (migration:90), but that is the write/audit/RPC-return path, not the sheet's direct-table read path — so 'zero representation in the estimate sheet's data flow' is accurate when scoped to the render read path. WHY UNDERSTATED: (a) the SELECT/mapper also omits `tags` entirely (verified: toEstimateRowItem maps neither tags nor metadata), yet the plan's U1 keeps isBlankEstimateRowItem — which reads item.tags AND would read express metadata — so BOTH the retained blank classifier and the new express classifier are starved by this read path, not just metadata.expressComponent. (b) The plan never names ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT or toEstimateRowItem; U1 only lists types.ts 'if needed' (04-implementation-plan.md:64) and U3 lists metadata as a patch WRITE field (line 81), neither of which adds the read-side column/mapper. So the load-bearing repository change (add metadata+tags to SELECT, map them in toEstimateRowItem, extend the type) is genuinely unaddressed. Severity high is correct.

### [MEDIUM] A6-F3 — `source` is a derived field, not a settable column — express and detached rows BOTH derive source='project'  _(type: inaccuracy)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:34-37
- **Plan claim:** Express row minimum representation includes `source = "project"` (listed alongside real columns like material/unit_cost), implying it is set on the row.
- **Ground truth:** There is no `source` column on estimate_row_items (table def 20260518090000:96-118). `source` is derived in the mapper: `source: row.source_library_item_id ? "library" : "project"` (workspace-repository.ts:1658). The type EstimateRowItemSource is 'project' | 'supplier' | 'library' (types.ts:1). Consequently express rows, detached rows, AND legacy blank placeholders all have source_library_item_id=null and therefore all derive source='project'.
- **Detail:** Listing source alongside material/unit_cost in the 'minimum row representation' wrongly implies it is a stored, independently-set value. It is computed from source_library_item_id. This matters because the plan repeatedly uses source='project' as a discriminator (e.g. inclusion rules) when in fact it cannot distinguish express from detached from blank rows — only link_state and metadata can.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:96-118, src/lib/repositories/workspace-repository.ts:1658, src/lib/estimates/types.ts:1
- **Fix:** Clarify that `source` is derived from source_library_item_id presence and cannot be set directly, and that source='project' alone does not distinguish express / detached / blank rows. Lean on link_state + metadata.expressComponent for discrimination instead.

### [MEDIUM] A6-F4 — Library-to-project-specific path assumes a linked row, but unlink RPC rejects rows that aren't already linked  _(type: risk)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:71-75
- **Plan claim:** "Insert the library component into estimate_row_items ... Clear source_library_item_id ... Record detach metadata and history through unlink_estimate_row_item_from_library_with_history."
- **Ground truth:** unlink_estimate_row_item_from_library_with_history raises 'Estimate row item is not linked to a library item.' when source_library_item_id IS NULL (20260601184343:60-62), and the unlink itself is what clears source_library_item_id (line 72). It also requires a correct expected_row_revision (50-58).
- **Detail:** The plan's ordering ('clear source_library_item_id' then 'record via unlink RPC') is backwards/ambiguous: the row must be inserted WITH a source_library_item_id (linked) first, then the unlink RPC clears it and sets link_state='detached'. If the row is inserted already source-less, the unlink RPC will reject it. The detach flow is: insert linked → call unlink RPC (which clears + detaches), not insert detached then call unlink.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260601184343_unlink_estimate_row_items.sql:60-62, supabase/migrations/20260601184343_unlink_estimate_row_items.sql:71-79, supabase/migrations/20260601184343_unlink_estimate_row_items.sql:50-58
- **Fix:** Reorder the detach steps: (1) insert the row linked to the library item with link_state linked-*, (2) call unlink_estimate_row_item_from_library_with_history with the correct row_revision, which atomically clears source_library_item_id, sets link_state='detached', and merges detach metadata. Remove the implication that the caller clears source_library_item_id separately.

### [LOW] A6-F5 — Live-evidence and data-model docs omit the is_platform_admin() arm of the estimate_row_items policies  _(type: missing-evidence)_
- **Plan loc:** evidence/live-supabase-evidence.md:35-39 (also 02-data-model:125)
- **Plan claim:** estimate_row_items: "read by viewers/managers through can_access_estimate or can_edit_estimate; insert/update/delete by managers through can_edit_estimate."
- **Ground truth:** The actual policies also include an is_platform_admin() bypass on every command: SELECT (20260614144136:91-93), INSERT (100-101), UPDATE (108-113), DELETE (120-121). The 'to public' role is then narrowed to 'authenticated' by 20260614144712:11-14.
- **Detail:** The claim is correct as far as it goes but incomplete: every estimate_row_items policy ORs in is_platform_admin(). For an audit doc that asserts policy shape, omitting the platform-admin override understates who can write rows. Low severity because it does not change the express design, but it is an inaccuracy of completeness in a doc meant to be ground truth.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260614144136_command_split_core_public_policies.sql:86-122, supabase/migrations/20260614144712_narrow_private_rls_policies_to_authenticated.sql:11-14
- **Fix:** Add is_platform_admin() to the documented policy predicates and note that the policies were narrowed to the authenticated role on 2026-06-14, so the evidence reflects the current (post-72628d46) policy set.

### [LOW] A6-F6 — Plan misses the area-node constraint trigger / insert RPC node.kind='area' guard on estimate_row_items  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:30-50
- **Plan claim:** Express rows live in estimate_row_items with normal row columns; no mention of node-kind placement constraints.
- **Ground truth:** insert_estimate_row_item_with_history requires the target node to be kind='area' (20260524050746:344-352), and a constraint trigger estimate_row_items_enforce_area_node enforces this at the table level (20260520171505:152-153).
- **Detail:** Express authoring via Add row must target an area node; rows cannot be attached to non-area (e.g. group/section) nodes. The plan's data model never surfaces this placement constraint, which is relevant to where the Add-row express entrypoint can appear in the tree.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:344-352, supabase/migrations/20260520171505_enforce_two_kind_estimate_nodes.sql:152-153
- **Fix:** Note the area-node placement constraint in the data-model doc so the UI entrypoint design respects that express rows can only be inserted under area-kind nodes.

### [INFO] A6-F7 — event_type is unconstrained free text — adding a new express activity event needs no events-table migration (supports the plan but unstated)  _(type: strength)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:52
- **Plan claim:** "Express rows should record the existing row_added activity unless a new activity event is deliberately added with composer support."
- **Ground truth:** estimate_sheet_events.event_type is `text not null check (char_length(trim(event_type)) > 0)` (20260524033151:17) — there is no enum or value whitelist. The current event types ('row_added','row_blank_added','row_updated','row_unlinked', etc.) are produced solely by RPC code, not enforced by a constraint.
- **Detail:** This is favorable for the plan: introducing a dedicated event type (e.g. 'row_express_added') requires zero schema change to the events table — only an RPC body change plus client/composer support. The plan's 'no migration required unless RPC rejects richer patches' gate (05:72) is therefore consistent for the activity-classification fix, since fixing line 422 is a CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION (a migration to the function, but no table/constraint change).
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:17, supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:422
- **Fix:** Make explicit that the insert-RPC fix is itself a migration (CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION) even though no table/constraint changes; and that adding a new event_type is constraint-free. This tightens the 05 'no migration required' wording, which currently reads as if even the classification fix might be migration-free.

### [LOW] A6-F8 — row_revision optimistic-lock mechanics are correct but the plan never documents the conflict contract for express material edits  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md:82
- **Plan claim:** 05 Release Risks: "Updating material fields through the same queue as numeric fields may create stale revision conflicts if rapid edits are split into multiple RPCs."
- **Ground truth:** update RPC enforces expected_row_revision == old_row.row_revision and raises 'estimate_sheet_row_revision_conflict' (errcode P0001) with a JSON detail payload, incrementing row_revision by 1 on success (20260524050746:489-497, 519). row_revision is integer not null default 1 check (>0) (20260524033151:9).
- **Detail:** The plan correctly anticipates the conflict risk at a high level, but does not document the actual contract the express editor must honor: each successful update bumps row_revision by exactly 1, every patch must carry the latest expected_row_revision, and a mismatch yields a P0001 error whose detail JSON includes currentRowRevision for retry. Express components add more editable fields per row, increasing the chance of split-RPC revision races; the implementation needs an explicit re-read/refresh-on-conflict strategy.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:489-497, supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:519, supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:9
- **Fix:** Document the row_revision contract (one-bump-per-success, P0001 conflict error shape with currentRowRevision) and require the express editor to either batch all express field edits into a single update RPC call or refresh row_revision from the conflict detail and retry, to avoid lost updates across the multi-field express form.

**Missing documents proposed by this lane:**
- **Express Insert RPC Migration Spec** — Pin down the exact CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION change to insert_estimate_row_item_with_history so the blank-vs-express classification is unambiguous and stays in sync with the client.
    - Current behavior: line 422 classifies on source_library_item_id IS NULL
    - Target predicate for 'row_blank_added': material = BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL OR metadata.tags ? 'manual-row-placeholder'
    - New event type decision: reuse 'row_added' for express vs introduce 'row_express_added' (event_type is unconstrained text, no table migration)
    - Composer/activity-history support for any new event type
    - Backfill/compat: ensure legacy blank rows still classify correctly
    - Test matrix: blank placeholder, express row, detached-then-reinserted row, linked row
- **Estimate Row Metadata Surface Change Spec** — Specify the repository and type changes needed to expose metadata.expressComponent to the estimate sheet, since the plan's identity model depends on it.
    - Add metadata to ESTIMATE_ROW_ITEM_SELECT (estimate-repository.ts:45)
    - Map metadata in toEstimateRowItem and round-trip in toEstimateRowItemUpdate
    - Extend EstimateRowItem type (types.ts) with metadata/expressComponent fields
    - Define the metadata.expressComponent contract and how tags coexist inside metadata
    - Confirm update RPC merge strategy so partial patches never drop expressComponent/tags/detach provenance
