    - Tab order within the crowded cell: editable fields vs icon rail ordering, tabbability of drag/details during edit, last-field->quantity continuity
    - Matching keyboard regression test list to add to the QA matrix
- **09-express-editor-state-and-copy-spec.md** — Provide the single source of truth for all interaction states (B2-F3) and microcopy/announcements (B2-F5) the editor and aria-live regions must share.
    - State table enumerating all ~14 states (new/empty/clean/dirty/saving/saved/failed/detached/linked/linked-overridden/source-changed/promoting/draft-insert-failed/loading) with visual treatment, focusability, and per-state aria-live announcement string
    - Exact field labels (Component name/description/Unit/Unit cost/Supplier) qualified by row name, and the aria-describedby error-id wiring
    - Empty-state placeholder copy for a new express row before first keystroke (replacing the blank-slot copy)
    - Failed-save inline error sentence, retry/delete-draft button labels, and promotion-confirmation copy
    - Contrast/color tokens per state (#5D5D5D or stronger for load-bearing meta, accent-soft+accent ring for active/replace, never #7F7F7F/secondary-60) and non-color status cues + prefers-reduced-motion notes

---

## Lane B3-blast-radius — readiness 58/100
**Summary:** I traced every consumer of the five blast-radius vectors (blank template, update contract, metadata.expressComponent, isBlankEstimateRowItem, link_state/source fields) against ground truth. The plan's audit line numbers are largely accurate (drop-zones:36, EstimateSheetContent:220/1700/1960, actions.ts:2493/2561, use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:49/70, migration JSON-replace behavior all verified), and the plan shows genuine awareness of the hardest interactions (RPC JSON wholesale-replace, row_blank_added classification, tab query-key drift for the icon bug). However the blast radius is materially incomplete. The single most serious gap: the metadata-identity decision (KTD2/U1) cannot work as written because the domain EstimateRowItem (domain/estimate-tree.ts:25) and UI EstimateRowItem (estimates/types.ts:81) carry no metadata/expressComponent field, and the main estimate-tree read mapping (workspace-repository.ts:1650) only extracts tags/laborUnitCost/scopeStatus from metadata — none of these read surfaces appear in any U-unit. Sixteen lib files touch estimate_row_items; the plan omits the AI package-builder write path (apply.ts writes metadata/link_state wholesale), project-charts-repository, publication/snapshot + share page (express rows publish to the public proposal), and the clearRowComponent→createBlankEstimateRowTemplate revert path (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2065) that turns an express row back into a legacy blank on Clear/Move. The move/replace path (applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem) silently drops inline express text edits. Test-fixture blast radius is under-listed (version-control-ui.test.tsx asserts on row_blank_added rendering but is not in U2/U8). Readiness for blast-radius coverage is moderate: the design choices are sound but several load-bearing surfaces are unenumerated, so an implementer following the U-unit file lists would miss them.

**Dimension scores:** Blank-template blast radius 55; Update-contract widening blast radius 70; metadata.expressComponent blast radius 40; isBlankEstimateRowItem classification blast radius 68; link_state/source-field consumers blast radius 50

**Strengths:**
- Plan correctly identifies the hardest server-side hazard: the update RPC replaces metadata and source_snapshot wholesale (verified at migration 20260524033151...sql:478-479), and KTD7/RSK3 require server-merged JSON before client uses richer fields.
- Plan correctly identifies that the insert RPC classifies any source-less row as row_blank_added (verified at migration line 384: 'case when inserted_row.source_library_item_id is null then row_blank_added else row_added'), and RSK9/U2/U8 require narrowing this to true blanks.
- Plan's audit line numbers are accurate to ground truth: EstimateSheetContent.tsx:220 (template), :1700 (optimistic insert), :1960 (insertBlankRow), actions.ts:2493 (insert) / :2561 (update), use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:49 (narrow update input) / :70 (optimistic patch) all verified.
- Plan anticipates the tab query-key drift as a candidate icon-bug cause (audit 01:71), which matches the real tab-aware key selection in use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:137/175/200/209.
- U3 correctly lists estimate-row-tree-patch.ts as needing widening; verified that EstimateRowItemPatch (estimate-row-tree-patch.ts:3-12) and patchEstimateRowItem (line 32-45) lack material/description/unit/supplier/metadata/sourceSnapshot, so optimistic inline edits genuinely require this change.
- U8 correctly claims project-source reuse rekeys row ids: cloneLibraryItemForEstimate (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352) calls createEstimateRowItemId() and spreads ...libraryItem, so express metadata survives the rekey.

**Findings:**

### [HIGH] B3-F1 — Metadata-identity (KTD2) is unreadable client-side: domain + UI EstimateRowItem types and the estimate-tree read mapping carry no metadata/expressComponent, and none are in any U-unit  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md KTD2 (line 34) and U1 (lines 61-66); 02-data-model lines 39-48, 95-96
- **Plan claim:** KTD2: 'Express identity is metadata, not a new enum: Use metadata.expressComponent and normal row source/link fields first.' U1 adds 'express helpers based on metadata.expressComponent or tags.'
- **Ground truth:** The UI EstimateRowItem type (src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81-136) has no `metadata` and no `expressComponent` field. The domain EstimateRowItem (src/lib/domain/estimate-tree.ts:25-44) also has no metadata field. The main estimate-tree read mapping (src/lib/repositories/workspace-repository.ts:1636-1683) reads row.metadata (line 1637) but only extracts `tags` (1643), `laborUnitCost` (1647), `scopeStatus` (1648) — it never surfaces metadata.expressComponent. So a client-side express helper cannot read metadata.expressComponent without also widening the type and the mapping. The `tags` fallback path IS viable (tags already flow from metadata.tags at line 1643), but the plan never picks a mechanism or lists the read surfaces.
- **Detail:** This is the linchpin of the no-new-table decision. As written, U1's metadata.expressComponent path is non-functional client-side because the data never reaches the tree. The implementer following U1/U3 file lists (which name activity-history.tsx, cells.tsx, types.ts 'if needed', estimate-row-tree-patch.ts) would not touch workspace-repository.ts or domain/estimate-tree.ts and would discover this only at integration time.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81-136, src/lib/domain/estimate-tree.ts:25-44, src/lib/repositories/workspace-repository.ts:1636-1648, src/lib/repositories/workspace-repository.ts:1650-1683
- **Fix:** Add an explicit U-unit (or extend U3) that: (1) decides express identity mechanism — either reuse the already-passed-through `tags` (cheapest, no read change) or add an `expressComponent` field to src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81 AND extract it in src/lib/repositories/workspace-repository.ts:1650; (2) names workspace-repository.ts and domain/estimate-tree.ts as read-side blast-radius surfaces; (3) adds a read-mapping test asserting expressComponent/tags survive the round trip.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Independently confirmed against ground truth. The UI EstimateRowItem type carries no `metadata` and no `expressComponent` field (src/lib/estimates/types.ts:81-136; fields stop at laborUnitCost/scopeStatus, no metadata blob). The domain EstimateRowItem has no metadata field either (src/lib/domain/estimate-tree.ts:25-44). The main read mapping reads row.metadata (workspace-repository.ts:1637) but extracts ONLY tags (1643), laborUnitCost (1647), scopeStatus (1648) and never surfaces metadata.expressComponent into the returned object (the return literal spans 1650-1683 and contains no express field). So a client-side express helper keyed on metadata.expressComponent is non-functional until the mapping AND both EstimateRowItem types are widened. The original finding's sub-claim that the `tags` fallback is viable is also correct: tags already flow from metadata.tags at 1643 and are typed at types.ts:109. Strengthening the finding: the plan does not just omit the read surfaces from U1/U3 file lists (U1 files = activity-history.tsx, cells.tsx, types.ts 'if needed' at 04-implementation-plan.md:64) — the data-model doc affirmatively claims the no-table choice means 'no new ... generated type surface' (02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:113), which is the precise assumption this gap falsifies. A grep of the whole plan pack shows zero references to workspace-repository.ts or domain/estimate-tree.ts. Severity high is correct: this is the linchpin of the no-new-table decision and would only surface at integration time. Caveat for accuracy: U3 (04:80-81) does have the implementer touch metadata write-side via estimate-row-tree-patch.ts/actions.ts, but I confirmed estimate-row-tree-patch.ts contains no metadata/expressComponent handling today, and write-side does not solve the client read mapping. The original line citations are all accurate.

### [HIGH] B3-F2 — clearRowComponent reverts an express row to a legacy blank placeholder via createBlankEstimateRowTemplate — a second template consumer the audit underweights and U2/U8 never address  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md lines 14-16; 04-implementation-plan.md Sources lines 132-133, R8 line 26
- **Plan claim:** Audit lists createBlankEstimateRowTemplate consumers as insertBlankRow (220/1960) and optimistic insert (1700). Sources list only EstimateSheetContent.tsx:220 and :1700. R8 says drag/drop replace, move, clear, delete remain functional.
- **Ground truth:** createBlankEstimateRowTemplate() is called at EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2065 inside clearRowComponent, NOT only at 1962. moveRowComponent (line 2093) is replaceRowItem(target, movedItem) + clearRowComponent(source) — so clearing OR moving an express row replaces it with a blank placeholder template. An express row that is cleared becomes a legacy blank, silently discarding its express identity/metadata; moving an express row turns the source slot into a blank.
- **Detail:** The audit (01:14) only mentions the insert path. Under Express Components, 'Clear' semantics are ambiguous: should clearing an express row delete it, blank it, or keep the express shell? The plan never specifies, and the default behavior (revert to blank) interacts with the row_blank_added classification fix and the Project-folder inclusion rules.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2064-2066, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2093-2097, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:1962
- **Fix:** Add clearRowComponent (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2065) and moveRowComponent (2093) to U2/U8 blast radius. Decide and document Clear semantics for express rows, and ensure the blank template used by Clear is still classified as blank (not express) so it is excluded from the Project folder projection.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Confirmed with one minor line correction. createBlankEstimateRowTemplate() is indeed called at EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2065 inside clearRowComponent (clearRowComponent = useStableCallback at 2064; body calls replaceRowItem(rowItemId, createBlankEstimateRowTemplate()) at 2065) — a third consumer beyond the insert path. rg confirms exactly three call sites: 1962 (insert via insertBlankRow), 2065 (clearRowComponent), and the definition at 220. moveRowComponent (2093-2097) is replaceRowItem(target, movedItem) at 2095 followed by clearRowComponent(source) at 2096 — so clearing OR moving an express row turns the source slot into a legacy blank placeholder, silently discarding express identity. Minor inaccuracy in the original evidence: it cites EstimateSheetContent.tsx:1962 for the insert call (correct) but the audit it references uses ':1960' for that path (01-current-state-audit.md:14) — 1960 is where the insertBlankRow useCallback begins; the createBlankEstimateRowTemplate() call itself is at 1962. This does not weaken the finding. The audit's coverage gap is verified: 01-current-state-audit.md 'Add Row Flow' (lines 9-24) enumerates createBlankEstimateRowTemplate only in the insert path (14-16) and nowhere mentions clearRowComponent or moveRowComponent as consumers; U2 (04:68-75) and U8 (04:113-118) never address clear/move semantics for express rows. Severity high upheld: 'Clear' semantics for an express row (delete vs blank vs keep shell) are unspecified, and the default revert-to-blank interacts with the row_blank_added classification fix (U2 history contract, 04:74) and the Project-folder inclusion rules (U6, 04:103 excludes blank placeholders).

### [HIGH] B3-F3 — Replace/Move drops inline express text edits: applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem only re-applies numeric editable fields  _(type: risk)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md R8 (line 26), U4 (line 88)
- **Plan claim:** R8: 'Existing drag/drop replace, move, clear, delete... remain functional.' U4 says 'Keep drag/drop replace target handling.'
- **Ground truth:** applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:398-416) parses only quantity, contingencyPercentage, and miscCost from editableRow and spreads ...libraryItem for everything else. It is used by replaceRowItem (line 2039) which both Replace-with-library and Move (via moveRowComponent line 2095) route through. The editable express text fields (material/description/unit/unit_cost) live in EditableRowValue/local state and would be lost because they are not applied here — only the persisted libraryItem fields survive.
- **Detail:** If U3/U4 add material/description/unit/unit_cost to EditableRowValue for inline editing, applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem becomes a silent data-loss surface on Replace and Move of an express row with unsaved inline edits. This is a direct consequence of widening the editable model and is not in any U-unit's file detail beyond cells.tsx/row-table.tsx.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:398-416, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2035-2062, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:2093-2097
- **Fix:** List applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:398) in U3/U4 blast radius. Either carry the new editable text fields through it or ensure inline edits are committed before a Replace/Move can occur. Add a regression test: edit express name, then move the row, assert name persists or commit-before-move is enforced.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Confirmed as a forward-looking risk, correctly scoped. applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:398-416) parses only quantity (401), contingencyPercentage (402), and miscCost (403) from editableRow, then spreads ...libraryItem for everything else (408) — material/description/unit/itemCost are taken from libraryItem only, never from the editable model. It is the single chokepoint for replaceRowItem (called at 2039) which both Replace-with-library and Move route through (moveRowComponent -> replaceRowItem at 2095). I independently verified the premise: EditableRowValue today holds ONLY quantity, contingencyMode, contingencyPercentage, miscCost (src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/editable-row-model.ts:44-49) — no text fields. So the risk is genuinely conditional on U3/U4 widening EditableRowValue to carry express text edits (U3 widens the persisted patch at 04:79-82; U4 builds the inline editor at 04:84-90), and at that point an in-flight (un-committed-to-tree) inline edit on an express row would be silently dropped on Replace/Move because applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem won't re-apply it. Important precision the original finding gets right but should be read carefully: for a MOVE, the source row's already-committed tree fields DO survive because movedItem is the full in-tree item object (cells.tsx:156/171 serialize {rowItemId, item}); the loss is specifically of unsaved editable-state edits, exactly as the finding's 'with unsaved inline edits' qualifier states. One nuance worth flagging: in replaceRowItem the editable row looked up is previousRows[rowItemId] = the TARGET slot's editable values (2038), so on a Replace the relevant unsaved edits are the target's, and on a Move the target id receives movedItem — meaning a destination cell mid-edit also loses its text edits. Severity high is appropriate given U3/U4 are in-scope and no U-unit lists applyEditableValuesToLibraryItem or EstimateSheetContent.tsx beyond U2/U5 generic mentions; this is a silent data-loss surface created as a direct consequence of widening the editable model.

### [MEDIUM] B3-F4 — AI package-builder writes estimate_row_items.metadata/link_state/source_snapshot wholesale — a parallel write path that contends with metadata.expressComponent, omitted entirely  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md KTD2 (line 34), U3 (lines 77-82); 02-data-model lines 39-52
- **Plan claim:** The plan treats estimate_row_items writes as flowing only through insert/update/replace/detach actions; KTD2 assumes metadata.expressComponent is a stable per-row object.
- **Ground truth:** src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts builds and writes estimate_row_items rows directly with its own metadata (apply.ts:479, 539, 590), link_state (490, 551), and source_snapshot (457, 515), via buildAiMetadata and inserts into estimate_row_items (apply.ts:948, 1269). This is a second, independent producer of rows and of the metadata JSONB the plan wants to own for expressComponent.
- **Detail:** AI-applied rows carry AI metadata, not express metadata. The classification helpers (U1) must not misclassify AI rows as express or blank, and the metadata.expressComponent convention must coexist with AI metadata keys in the same JSONB. The plan never names this surface.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts:443-490, src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts:508-551, src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts:948, src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts:1269-1287
- **Fix:** Add src/lib/ai/package-builder/apply.ts to the blast-radius/coexistence list. Specify that express classification keys are namespaced under metadata.expressComponent and that U1 helpers return false for AI-produced rows. Add a classifier unit test using an AI-metadata row fixture.

The public share view renders the row thumbnail from source_snapshot.imageUrl (src/components/share/SharePageTabs.tsx:491, 497-501). Express rows — including empty/zero-cost ones — will be published as real line items and their images shown publicly. Neither publication/* nor SharePageTabs.tsx is named in any U-unit.

'Proposal/export' in U8 is the Excel export path, distinct from publication/share.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/publication/actions.ts:376, src/lib/publication/actions.ts:436-450, src/lib/publication/snapshot.ts:77-105, src/components/share/SharePageTabs.tsx:491-501
- **Fix:** Add src/lib/publication/actions.ts, snapshot.ts, and src/components/share/SharePageTabs.tsx to U8 blast radius. Add a test asserting an empty express row (placeholder material, zero cost) is filtered at publish, and that a real express row with imageUrl renders in the share thumbnail.

### [MEDIUM] B3-F8 — Per-row memoization comparator and computed-item memo not analyzed for inline-editor inputs; risk that express keystrokes don't re-render or that the comparator misses new editable fields  _(type: risk)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md lines 37-41; 05-readiness-qa-matrix line 81
- **Plan claim:** Audit notes row-table.tsx:61 'compares row table props by array and per-row editable state' and :88 fixes grid columns. R8 says row virtualization remains functional. Release risk: 'Row virtualization may mismeasure active express editor height.'
- **Ground truth:** areEstimateRowItemsTablePropsEqual (row-table.tsx:61-86) compares prev.items by reference and per-row editableRows[item.id] and selectedRowIds membership only. The per-row EstimateRowItemTableRow is React.memo with default shallow comparison (row-table.tsx:133) and its computedItem useMemo is keyed on [editableRow, item] (line 157-180), which currently spreads only numeric editable fields. New inline express text edits must flow through editableRow (so the comparator at line 82 detects them) and through computedItem, or keystrokes will be dropped by memoization. The plan flags height mismeasurement but not the comparator/computed-item dependency on the new editable fields.
- **Detail:** This is the recent-commit memoization surface (72628d46). If express material/description/unit/unit_cost are stored outside editableRows (e.g. separate state), the line-82 comparator will not invalidate the memo and inline edits will appear frozen until an unrelated re-render. Conversely if stored in editableRows, getInitialEditableRow/updateEditableRowValue and computedItem must carry them.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/row-table.tsx:61-86, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/row-table.tsx:133-180, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/row-table.tsx:244-252
- **Fix:** Add explicit memoization analysis to U4: route express text fields through EditableRowValue so the line-82 comparator detects them, extend getInitialEditableRow/updateEditableRowValue and the computedItem memo (line 157) accordingly, and add a perf/correctness test that an inline name keystroke re-renders only the edited row.

[] verbatim into reusable sources with no blank/express/linked-overridden filtering. If the Project folder reuses this collector, blank and linked-overridden rows leak in, contradicting the inclusion rules.

- **Evidence:** src/lib/reusable-sources/source-collectors.ts:76-98, src/lib/reusable-sources/source-collectors.ts:129-155
- **Fix:** Either build a purpose-specific project-row query (the plan's preferred 'purpose-built' option, U6 line 103) or add explicit blank/linked-overridden filtering to a Project-folder-specific consumer of source-collectors.ts. Name src/lib/reusable-sources/source-collectors.ts in U6 blast radius.

## Lane B4-downstream-impact — readiness 74/100
**Summary:** I ground-truth-verified every downstream surface the plan names plus several it omits, reading the actual RPC bodies, repositories, and renderers at commit 72628d46. The plan's single most important downstream concern is real and confirmed: the active insert RPC (insert_estimate_row_item_with_history, migration 20260524050746:422) classifies any source-less row as `row_blank_added`, and the activity composer (activity-composer.ts:83-84) renders that event as "added an empty row slot" with the material name discarded entirely — so a named, priced express row would be mislabeled in history. The plan correctly identifies this (RSK9/U8) and proposes a correct fix. The totals/calculation engine claims are strongly supported: line totals (estimate-row-total.ts, the SQL line-total fn, dashboard, list-summaries RPC, publication snapshot, and proposal export) ALL derive money from row-level quantity/unit_cost/misc_cost/contingency with zero dependence on source linkage, so express rows aggregate correctly everywhere. Project-source reuse is also already handled: the subtree-copy RPC (20260613134852:195,219) rekeys row ids with gen_random_uuid() and merges (preserves) metadata + source_snapshot, so express metadata survives a copy with no plan changes needed. However I found concrete gaps the plan does NOT address: (1) the published client-facing image is extracted ONLY from source_snapshot.imageUrl (publication/actions.ts:436) and the dashboard/charts category+supplier bucketing reads source_snapshot.{categoryNodeId,supplierId} (project-dashboard-repository.ts:170-173), so an express image stored on metadata and a source-less express row would lose its thumbnail / land in an "Uncategorized" donut bucket; (2) the proposal/BOQ section assignment uses row.category||row.discipline (build-proposal-export-model.ts:130), so default express rows fall into the "Uncategorized"/Misc division; (3) the AI read-context surface (ai/read-context.ts:484) is a second place that mislabels row_blank_added as "Blank row added" and is never mentioned. Two plan statements are inaccurate: U8's "dashboard aggregation from source snapshot" (totals are from row fields, not source_snapshot) and Q16's reliance on "existing export tests" without acknowledging the category/division placement change.

**Dimension scores:** Downstream coverage completeness 68; Accuracy of downstream claims 72; Totals/calculation correctness 92; Cited evidence precision (line numbers) 90; Risk identification for downstream 70

**Strengths:**
- The plan's central downstream risk (RSK9/U8: source-less express inserts mislabeled as blank-row activity) is REAL and precisely confirmed in code: insert RPC at supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:422 sets event_type='row_blank_added' when source_library_item_id IS NULL, and activity-composer.ts:83-84 renders that as 'added an empty row slot' discarding the material name. The plan's proposed fix (narrow row_blank_added to true blanks or use row_added) is the correct remedy.
- The totals/calculation claim is well-founded across the whole app: src/lib/estimates/estimate-row-total.ts:22-29, the SQL estimate_row_line_total, project-dashboard-repository.ts:114-117, project_estimate_list_summaries RPC (20260613201251:46-50), publication snapshot.ts:62-69, and proposal build-proposal-export-model.ts:121-129 ALL compute money from row-level quantity/unit_cost/misc_cost/contingency with no dependence on source linkage — express rows feed totals correctly everywhere with no engine changes.
- The project-source reuse claim (U8/Q14) is already satisfied by existing code: insert_estimate_subtree_into_estimate_with_history (20260613134852:195) rekeys each copied row with gen_random_uuid(), and lines 200/219 merge-preserve source_snapshot AND metadata (coalesce(row_item.metadata,'{}') || provenance), so metadata.expressComponent survives a copy. The in-tree clone path cloneLibraryItemForEstimate (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352-369) likewise rekeys id and spreads ...libraryItem to preserve metadata.
- The plan's cited source line numbers are accurate: EstimateSheetContent.tsx:220 (createBlankEstimateRowTemplate) is exact; cells.tsx:79 (canMakeProjectSpecific gate) is exact; cells.tsx:179 (blank material slot) is exact. canMakeProjectSpecific correctly returns false for source-less express rows, so express rows won't wrongly show the make-project-specific action.
- The update RPC already accepts the express material fields the plan needs (material, description, supplier, unit, unitCost at 20260524050746:504-510), so U3's persistence is mostly a client-side widening, lowering risk. The plan's KTD7/U3 claim that metadata and source_snapshot are REPLACED wholesale is confirmed exactly at lines 517-518.

**Findings:**

### [INFO] B4-F1 — Confirmed: insert RPC labels every source-less row as row_blank_added; composer renders it as 'empty row slot' ignoring the material name  _(type: strength)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 (lines 113-118), U2 history contract (line 74); 06-review-and-risk-register.md RSK9 (line 25)
- **Plan claim:** U8/RSK9: 'Current insert RPC classifies source-less rows as row_blank_added; express insert history must use row_added unless a new event is added.'
- **Ground truth:** insert RPC: source_library_item_id IS NULL => 'row_blank_added'. Composer: row_blank_added => 'added an empty row slot.' (material discarded).
- **Detail:** The plan's headline downstream concern is verified true. The active insert RPC body (newest create-or-replace of insert_estimate_row_item_with_history) sets event_type via `case when inserted_row.source_library_item_id is null then 'row_blank_added' else 'row_added' end`. Express rows are source-less by design, so they WILL be tagged row_blank_added. The activity composer's estimateTitle() then returns the literal string 'added an empty row slot.' for that event type and never references the material — so a user who authored a named, priced express component would see history say they 'added an empty row slot.' This is exactly RSK9/U8. The plan's fix (narrow row_blank_added to true placeholders, or use row_added for express) is correct and addresses the composer title.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524050746_add_estimate_row_contingency_percentage.sql:422, src/lib/activity/activity-composer.ts:83, src/lib/activity/activity-composer.ts:84
- **Fix:** Keep U2/U8's fix but make it concrete: the cleanest fix is to change the insert RPC's classification so an express row (e.g. metadata.expressComponent present, OR material != placeholder label, OR non-zero cost) emits 'row_added', not 'row_blank_added'. Add a migration to U3's scope (the plan currently treats the migration as optional). Add a regression asserting an express insert produces a row_added event whose composed title contains the material name.

### [MEDIUM] B4-F2 — Inaccuracy: U8 says dashboard aggregates 'from source snapshot' — totals actually come from row-level fields, not source_snapshot  _(type: inaccuracy)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 Tests (line 118) and Details (line 117)
- **Plan claim:** 'dashboard aggregation from source snapshot' and 'Confirm source snapshots include image/pricing provenance'
- **Ground truth:** Dashboard totals = row-level quantity*unit_cost+misc+contingency (project-dashboard-repository.ts:114-117, 175-178). source_snapshot only feeds donut category/supplier bucket ids.
- **Detail:** U8's test bullet 'dashboard aggregation from source snapshot' and the Details line 'Confirm source snapshots include image/pricing provenance' imply the dashboard reads totals out of source_snapshot. The dashboard does NOT: getDashboardEstimateSeries computes totalCost from finite(row.quantity)*finite(row.unitCost)+misc+contingency, and loadEstimateRowFacts selects row-level quantity/unit_cost/contingency_percentage/misc_cost. source_snapshot is read ONLY to derive categoryNodeId/supplierId for the cost-mix donut bucketing (lines 164-174), not for any money. This is actually good news for express rows (their row-level unit_cost aggregates correctly), but the plan's framing is wrong and could mislead the implementer into thinking express rows must populate source_snapshot for totals to work.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/repositories/project-dashboard-repository.ts:114, src/lib/repositories/project-dashboard-repository.ts:149, src/lib/repositories/project-dashboard-repository.ts:170, src/lib/repositories/project-dashboard-repository.ts:175
- **Fix:** Reword U8 to: 'dashboard/list totals aggregate from row-level quantity/unit_cost/misc/contingency (no source linkage), so express rows count correctly; source_snapshot is only used for cost-mix category/supplier bucketing — see B4-F3.' Drop the implication that totals come from source_snapshot.

### [MEDIUM] B4-F3 — Gap: source-less express rows fall into an 'Uncategorized' bucket in the dashboard cost-mix donut and the proposal/BOQ division  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 (113-118); 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md Q16 (line 32)
- **Plan claim:** Plan asserts export/dashboard correctness for express rows (U8, Q16) but does not address category/section placement.
- **Ground truth:** Express rows (source-less, default empty category/discipline) bucket to 'Uncategorized' in charts (aggregate-project-charts.ts:349,365-366) and to the Misc division in proposal export (build-proposal-export-model.ts:130,208-215).
- **Detail:** Two grouping surfaces bucket rows by data that express rows lack by default. (1) Dashboard/charts category grouping reads row.categoryNodeId from source_snapshot.categoryNodeId; a source-less express row yields null => the aggregator buckets it as id 'uncategorized'/label 'Uncategorized' (aggregate-project-charts.ts:349,365-366). Supplier grouping falls back to the inline supplier text/normalized label (line 372-375). (2) Proposal export sectionLabel = sourceRow.category || sourceRow.discipline || 'Uncategorized' (build-proposal-export-model.ts:130), and an empty section sorts into the Misc division (divisionRank => MISC_DIVISION_RANK, lines 208-215). So every default express row lands in Uncategorized/Misc in both the client-facing donut and the exported BOQ. Totals are unaffected, but the categorization is. The plan never addresses how express rows acquire a category/discipline, so this is a real client-facing presentation gap.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/project-charts/aggregate-project-charts.ts:349, src/lib/project-charts/aggregate-project-charts.ts:365, src/lib/proposal-export/build-proposal-export-model.ts:130, src/lib/proposal-export/build-proposal-export-model.ts:208
- **Fix:** Add a U8 sub-item: decide whether the express template seeds a category/discipline (e.g. from the active node path or a default) and whether category/supplier should be persisted to source_snapshot for express rows so charts/export bucket them meaningfully instead of dumping all express rows into Uncategorized/Misc. Add a QA case: 'express row appears under a sensible (or explicitly Uncategorized) section in proposal export and cost-mix donut.'

### [HIGH] B4-F4 — Gap: published client-facing thumbnail is extracted ONLY from source_snapshot.imageUrl — an express image on metadata would not publish  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U4 (line 88), U8 (line 117)
- **Plan claim:** U4: 'Optional image in the first pass is URL/source set-clear only'; U8: 'Confirm source snapshots include image/pricing provenance'.
- **Ground truth:** Publish reads image from source_snapshot.imageUrl (publication/actions.ts:436); snapshot input only carries imageUrl sourced from source_snapshot (snapshot.ts:26-27,82); history card reads sourceSnapshot.imageUrl (activity-history.tsx:464).
- **Detail:** The publication (shared client link) path extracts each row's image with readStringMetadataValue(row.source_snapshot, 'imageUrl') at publish time (publication/actions.ts:436, fed into buildSnapshotRow). The publication snapshot builder also only accepts imageUrl on its row input and documents it as 'source_snapshot.imageUrl extracted at publish' (snapshot.ts:26-27,82). The audit/history component-snapshot card likewise reads sourceSnapshot.imageUrl (activity-history.tsx:464). U4 scopes the express image to 'URL/source set-clear only' but never specifies WHERE the express image is stored. If the express editor writes the image to metadata (the natural home for express identity per KTD2) rather than source_snapshot.imageUrl, the image will render in the live sheet but silently DISAPPEAR from the published proposal and from history snapshot cards. This is a concrete cross-surface break the plan does not flag.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/publication/actions.ts:436, src/lib/publication/snapshot.ts:26, src/lib/publication/snapshot.ts:82, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:464
- **Fix:** State explicitly in U4/U8 that the express image MUST be written to source_snapshot.imageUrl (not only metadata) so it survives publication and history snapshots. Add a QA case under Q6/Q16: 'publish an estimate containing an express row with an image; assert the snapshot row carries the image.' Remember source_snapshot is REPLACED wholesale by the update RPC (20260524050746:517), so the server must merge the image into a complete source_snapshot — this ties directly to KTD7/U3.
- **Adversarial verify:** `refuted` (sev→low) — The finding's three ground-truth citations are all CORRECT: publication reads each row image via readStringMetadataValue(row.source_snapshot, 'imageUrl') (publication/actions.ts:436, fed into the row map at 437-451), and rows are queried with only source_snapshot selected for image extraction (publication/actions.ts:376 — metadata is NOT even selected for rows, only for the project cover at 424-428). The snapshot builder input only accepts imageUrl, documented as 'source_snapshot.imageUrl extracted at publish' (snapshot.ts:26-27, used at 82). The history card reads sourceSnapshot.imageUrl (activity-history.tsx:464). So publication/history genuinely read image ONLY from source_snapshot.imageUrl. HOWEVER, the finding's load-bearing CONCLUSION — that this is an unflagged cross-surface break because 'the express editor writes the image to metadata (the natural home for express identity per KTD2)' — is refuted on two independent grounds. (1) The plan explicitly and repeatedly specifies source_snapshot as the express image home, NOT metadata: 02-data-model line 38 ('source_snapshot updated with row-level image and pricing provenance where present'), line 115 ('optional image should be represented as an existing image URL/source value in source_snapshot'), and U3 in 04-implementation-plan line 81 routes the image through a dedicated 'sourceSnapshot' typed patch field (not the 'metadata' field, which is listed separately). KTD2 (04 line 34) scopes ONLY express IDENTITY (metadata.expressComponent object) to metadata; it never says the image lives in metadata. The finding conflates the identity object with the image field. (2) The LIVE sheet reads the row image from the SAME source_snapshot.imageUrl field — workspace-repository.ts (imageUrl: n(sourceSnapshot.imageUrl)) and estimates/actions.ts:1673 (imageUrl: normalizeEstimateRowImageUrl(sourceSnapshot.imageUrl)). So if an implementer wrongly wrote the image to metadata, it would NOT render live either; the finding's stated failure mode ('renders in the live sheet but silently DISAPPEARS from the published proposal') is structurally impossible because live and published reads share one field. Finally, the plan DOES flag this exact surface: U8 (04 line 117) requires 'Confirm source snapshots include image/pricing provenance.' What remains is a far weaker, low-severity latent risk (the editor must in fact write to source_snapshot.imageUrl, and U3's server-side merge must not drop it) — and 04 lines 81-82 already mandate that 'unrelated metadata/source snapshot keys survive an express edit.' The original 'high / concrete cross-surface break the plan does not flag' framing overstates a hypothetical the plan actively steers away from; downgrade to low.

### [LOW] B4-F5 — Gap: AI read-context is a second surface that mislabels row_blank_added as 'Blank row added' and is not mentioned anywhere in the plan  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 (lines 113-118)
- **Plan claim:** U8 lists downstream surfaces to verify (history, export, dashboard, project sources) but not AI read-context.

This surface feeds the in-app AI assistant's view of estimate activity. Until the insert classification is fixed (B4-F1), express inserts will read as 'Blank row added' to the AI context just as they do in the UI. The plan's U8 enumerates history, dashboard, export, and project-sources but omits the AI read-context surface entirely. Low severity because the same root-cause fix (correct event classification) resolves it automatically, but the plan should at least acknowledge it so it gets a regression.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/ai/read-context.ts:484, src/lib/ai/read-context.ts:483
- **Fix:** Add the AI read-context (src/lib/ai/read-context.ts) to U8's list of surfaces verified by the event-classification fix, and note that fixing the insert RPC classification (B4-F1) resolves it with no extra code beyond a confirming assertion.

### [INFO] B4-F6 — Confirmed strength: project-source reuse already rekeys ids and preserves express metadata + source_snapshot server-side  _(type: strength)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 (line 117); 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md Q14 (line 30)
- **Plan claim:** U8/Q14: 'project-source reuse rekeys row ids' and 'copied express rows get fresh ids and preserve express metadata'.
- **Ground truth:** Subtree-copy RPC: id=gen_random_uuid() (:195), metadata merge-preserved (:219), source_snapshot merge-preserved (:200), unit_cost copied (:213). Client clone: createEstimateRowItemId()+spread (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:353,360).
- **Detail:** U8/Q14 claim project-source reuse rekeys row ids and preserves express metadata. Verified true and already implemented. insertProjectSourceIntoEstimate calls the RPC insert_estimate_subtree_into_estimate_with_history (reusable-sources/actions.ts:401-414), which inserts copied rows with gen_random_uuid() for the id (migration :195) and copies every field including unit_cost (:213), with metadata = coalesce(row_item.metadata,'{}') || provenance (:219-224) and source_snapshot likewise merge-preserved (:200-205). So metadata.expressComponent survives a copy with fresh ids and no plan-side work. The in-tree clone path cloneLibraryItemForEstimate (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352-369) also rekeys via createEstimateRowItemId() and spreads ...libraryItem, preserving metadata/sourceSnapshot/linkState. The plan's Q14 is satisfiable; this lowers risk for that unit.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260613134852_insert_estimate_subtree_rpc.sql:195, supabase/migrations/20260613134852_insert_estimate_subtree_rpc.sql:219, src/lib/reusable-sources/actions.ts:401, src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:352
- **Fix:** Note in U8 that this path needs no changes; the Q14 test should just assert metadata.expressComponent is present and the id differs after a project-source copy. No new RPC required for express reuse.

### [MEDIUM] B4-F7 — Inconsistency: audit-snapshot suppression and composer title disagree for express rows, so a partial fix leaves a mixed-message history card  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U8 (line 117); 05 Q15 (line 31)
- **Plan claim:** U8: 'history titles do not call express rows blank rows' (implies a renderer-side fix may suffice).
- **Ground truth:** Composer title is driven by event_type only (activity-composer.ts:83-84); audit-card suppression is driven by material+cost heuristics (activity-history.tsx:235-242). They diverge for a priced express row.
- **Detail:** The activity-history audit layer has TWO independent blank checks that can disagree for an express row. isBlankEstimateRowAuditEvent (activity-history.tsx:235-242) suppresses the component-snapshot CARD only when event is row_blank_added/row_deleted AND material==placeholder label OR (no source snapshot AND no cost). An express row has a real material and non-zero cost, so isBlankEstimateRowAuditSnapshot returns false and the snapshot card WOULD render. But the event TITLE comes from the composer, which keys purely on event_type=row_blank_added => 'added an empty row slot' regardless of material/cost (activity-composer.ts:83-84). Net result if the implementer only fixes the audit-card heuristic (cells/history side) but NOT the insert classification: the history entry reads 'added an empty row slot' as its title while ALSO showing a populated component snapshot card with the express material/price — a self-contradictory entry. The plan's correct fix is at insert classification (B4-F1); this finding stresses that fixing only the renderer heuristics is insufficient.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/activity/activity-composer.ts:83, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:235, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:312
- **Fix:** Make U8/Q15 explicit that the fix MUST change the insert-time event_type (so the composer title is correct), not just the renderer heuristic. Add an assertion that for an express insert, both the composed title AND the snapshot card are non-blank and reference the material.

### [LOW] B4-F8 — Missing-evidence: Q16 leans on 'existing export tests' but those tests do not cover express-row category/division placement or merge-consolidation collisions  _(type: missing-evidence)_
- **Plan loc:** 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md Q16 (line 32)

That may be desired, but it is unverified for express rows.
- **Evidence:** src/lib/proposal-export/build-proposal-export-model.ts:161, src/lib/proposal-export/build-proposal-export-model.ts:130, src/lib/proposal-export/build-proposal-export-model.ts:396
- **Fix:** Add an express-specific export test asserting: (1) an express row's total flows into the grand total, (2) its section/division placement is intentional (Uncategorized/Misc or a seeded category), and (3) two identical express rows either correctly consolidate or are intentionally kept distinct. Don't rely solely on 'existing export tests.'

## Lane B5-gaps-missing-docs — readiness 62/100
**Summary:** The 6-doc pack is technically sound and unusually well-grounded for a day-0 plan: I verified the load-bearing code anchors against commit 72628d46 and they hold (line numbers drift by a few lines but symbols/behaviors are correct), and the central no-new-table decision is fully supported by the live schema. Critically, I confirmed the underlying update RPC ALREADY accepts the full set of patch keys the feature needs (material, description, supplier, unit, unitCost, miscCost, metadata, sourceSnapshot via the row_patch ? 'key' per-key replace pattern at migration 20260524033151:466-479), so no migration is required to widen it — a fact the plan gestures at but never pins down precisely. However, for a MISSION-CRITICAL day-0 build, 6 docs are NOT sufficient: the pack lacks a concrete RPC/SQL contract doc (exact patch payloads, the per-key vs deep-merge truth, the UpdateEstimateRowItemResult return-shape gap, app itemCost vs RPC unitCost naming), a test inventory that maps each of the 3 existing tests that must change to specific new tests, an R/U/AE/Q traceability matrix (several requirements have no acceptance example or QA row), an edge-case/failure-mode catalog, and a rollout/migration runbook. The QA matrix (05) lists 21 scenarios but does not trace them back to requirements or to named test files, and the implementation units (04) reference files that need creating without a file-creation manifest. The plan is review-ready but not yet hand-off-to-implementer ready without 4-5 supplementary docs.

**Dimension scores:** Document sufficiency (count & coverage) 58; Ground-truth accuracy of claims 85; Implementer hand-off readiness 55; Risk & edge-case rigor 60; Traceability & QA mapping 50

**Strengths:**
- Code anchors are real and verifiable: createBlankEstimateRowTemplate (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:219), insertLibraryItem (:1699), insertBlankRow (:1960), canMakeProjectSpecific (cells.tsx:84), createCostLibraryComponent (component-library/actions.ts:431 calling create_cost_library_component RPC at :448), and TemplatesTree project-source functions all exist as described.
- The no-new-table primary decision is genuinely well-supported by live schema evidence: estimate_row_items carries every needed column and the update RPC already supports rich patches, so the architectural claim is not aspirational — it is verified.
- The plan correctly identified the row_blank_added history-classification trap (migration 20260524050746:422) and that express inserts must avoid it — this is a subtle, real branch in the insert RPC.
- 06-review-and-risk-register.md shows a real adversarial review pass with P1 findings traced to specific units (U2/U4/U6/U7), which is stronger than typical day-0 packs.
- The JSON-merge risk (RSK3, KTD7) is real: the RPC does per-top-level-key replacement, so a partial metadata patch overwrites the whole metadata object — the plan flags this correctly even if it under-specifies the exact merge mechanics.

**Findings:**

### [HIGH] B5-F1 — No concrete RPC/SQL contract doc — exact patch shapes, merge semantics, and return shapes are scattered and partly wrong on naming  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md U3; 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md 'Supabase Requirements'
- **Plan claim:** 04-implementation-plan.md U3 says 'Add typed patch fields for material, description, supplier, unit, itemCost, miscCost, sourceSnapshot, and metadata' and 'the server action reads the current row and writes complete merged metadata and source_snapshot, or the RPC accepts explicit merge patches.' 02 says 'Current row update RPC behavior replaces JSON values.'
- **Ground truth:** The update RPC (supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:466-479) already accepts patch keys material, description, supplier, unit, unitCost, miscCost, metadata, sourceSnapshot, linkState, sourceLibraryItemId etc. via 'row_patch ? key then ... else row_item.col end'. Critically the patch key is 'unitCost' (camelCase), NOT 'itemCost' as U3 lists — the app uses itemCost, the DB column is unit_cost, the patch key is unitCost. The merge is per-top-level-key REPLACE (not deep merge): metadata = case when row_patch ? 'metadata' then coalesce(row_patch->'metadata','{}') else row_item.metadata end. And UpdateEstimateRowItemResult (src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:100-110) returns ONLY rowId,nodeId,quantity,itemCost,contingencyPercentage,contingencyValue,miscCost,linkState,rowRevision — it returns NO material/description/supplier/unit/metadata, so the optimistic reconcile cannot read widened text fields back from the result today.
- **Detail:** For a mission-critical inline editor the implementer needs an exact contract, not prose. Three naming layers (app itemCost / column unit_cost / patch key unitCost) are not reconciled anywhere; an implementer following U3 literally would send a non-existent 'itemCost' patch key and the unit-cost edit would silently no-op. The result-shape gap (no text fields returned) means U3's 'server result reconciles all changed fields' test cannot pass without first widening UpdateEstimateRowItemResult — a concrete blocker the plan never names.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:466, supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:468, supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:478, src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:100, src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:2563
- **Fix:** Author a CONTRACT doc (see missingDocuments 'RPC and Row-Patch Contract'). It must state: (1) no migration needed to widen the update RPC; (2) the canonical key map app->patch->column for every field, fixing U3's itemCost/unitCost error; (3) that merge is per-top-level-key replace and metadata/source_snapshot must be fully merged server-side before patch; (4) that UpdateEstimateRowItemResult must be widened to return text/metadata fields and the tree-patch reconcile updated accordingly; (5) exact promotion RPC shape for U7.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Independently re-verified every technical sub-claim and all hold. (1) The update RPC at supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:464-479 accepts patch keys via 'row_patch ? key then ... else row_item.col'; the unit-cost patch key is literally 'unitCost' (line 466: "unit_cost = case when row_patch ? 'unitCost'..."), NOT 'itemCost'. (2) The three-layer naming is real and unreconciled: app domain field is 'itemCost' (actions.ts:104 result type, :351 readNumber(row.unitCost) -> itemCost), DB column is unit_cost, RPC patch key is unitCost (insert path actions.ts:2546 maps 'unitCost: libraryItem.itemCost'). An implementer following U3 (04-implementation-plan.md:81) literally would add an 'itemCost' patch field, which the RPC's 'row_patch ? \'itemCost\'' test never matches, so the unit-cost edit silently no-ops. (3) Merge is per-top-level-key REPLACE, not deep merge: metadata (line 479) = "case when row_patch ? 'metadata' then coalesce(row_patch->'metadata','{}'::jsonb) else row_item.metadata end" and source_snapshot (line 478) replaces wholesale. (4) The result-shape blocker is real and the plan never names it: UpdateEstimateRowItemResult (actions.ts:100-110) and readHistoryRowResult (actions.ts:337-358) return ONLY rowId, nodeId, quantity, itemCost, contingencyPercentage, contingencyValue, miscCost, linkState, rowRevision — NO material/description/supplier/unit/metadata/sourceSnapshot. The numeric-only contract is consistent across all layers: hook UpdateEstimateRowInput (use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:49-57), action input UpdateEstimateRowItemInput (actions.ts:80-89), and the rowPatch built at actions.ts:2564-2569. So U3's test 'server result reconciles all changed fields' (04:82) cannot pass without first widening readHistoryRowResult + UpdateEstimateRowItemResult — a concrete blocker the plan only gestures at in prose (RSK3 in 06:19 names 'result shape does not return widened fields' but the plan body never states the itemCost-vs-unitCost patch-key trap). Severity high is correct: the naming trap is a silent-no-op landmine and the result gap blocks a stated test.

### [HIGH] B5-F2 — No requirement<->unit<->acceptance-example<->QA traceability matrix; several R/U have no AE or Q  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md Requirements/Units/Acceptance Examples; 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md Scenario Matrix
- **Plan claim:** 04 lists R1-R11, U1-U8, AE1-AE6; 05 lists Q1-Q21. INDEX.md implies these are the complete handoff.

R9/R10/R11 map to AE2a/AE6 partially but R9 (no new table) has no test. U8 (history/export/dashboard/project-sources verification) maps to Q14/Q15/Q16 but there is no AE for dashboard aggregation. AE numbering also skips cleanly (AE2a inserted) but nothing maps AE<->Q<->U explicitly, so an implementer cannot tell which test proves which requirement.
- **Detail:** A mission-critical feature touching the core estimate editor needs an auditable grid proving every requirement is covered by at least one acceptance example AND one QA scenario AND one unit. The pack has the raw materials (R/U/AE/Q all exist) but no matrix, and the matrix would expose real holes: R8 regression coverage is the most dangerous — the express template swap (U2) changes the Add row path that is currently asserted by 3 tests, and nothing guarantees move/clear/delete still pass.
- **Evidence:** /Users/philiplewis/Documents/archestimator/.ai/03_plans/2026-06-15-Express-Components/04-implementation-plan.md, /Users/philiplewis/Documents/archestimator/.ai/03_plans/2026-06-15-Express-Components/05-readiness-qa-matrix.md
- **Fix:** Author a TRACEABILITY doc (see missingDocuments). Build an R x U x AE x Q grid, mark every empty cell, and add the missing coverage: an AE+Q for R8 regression (move/clear/delete/select/keyboard/virtualization unaffected by express template), an AE for dashboard aggregation (U8), and an explicit 'no new table' assertion strategy for R9.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — Re-checked the docs and the no-traceability-matrix gap is real. A targeted grep across 04/05/06 for any explicit R<->U<->AE<->Q cross-reference (maps/covers/prove/AE-to-Q linkage) returned ZERO hits — no doc ties a requirement to the unit, acceptance example, and QA scenario that prove it. R1-R11 (04:19-29), U1-U8 (04:61-118), AE1-AE6 plus the inserted AE2a (04:122-128), and Q1-Q21 (05:17-37) all exist as raw lists with no linking grid, exactly as F2 says. The most dangerous hole is confirmed: R8 (drag/drop replace, move, clear, delete, row selection, keyboard nav, virtualization remain functional, 04:26) has NO acceptance example and NO Q row; its only coverage is a browser-smoke line (05:68 'replace an express row with a library component, then clear it') and a virtualization release-risk note (05:81). That smoke line touches replace+clear only — move, delete, row selection, and keyboard-nav regression have zero coverage of any kind, automated or manual. This is the precise regression surface U2 disturbs (it rewrites the Add row path asserted by the existing blank-placeholder tests). Minor correction to F2's wording: it says R8 has 'only an indirect browser-smoke mention' — accurate, but I'd note the smoke does cover replace+clear, so the gap is specifically move/delete/selection/keyboard, not 100% of R8. Additional understatement to flag: F2 says 'U8 maps to Q14/Q15/Q16' — but U8's own listed test 'dashboard aggregation from source snapshot' (04:118) maps to NONE of Q14 (project-source reuse), Q15 (history/audit), or Q16 (proposal/export totals), so dashboard aggregation has neither a Q row nor an AE, a slightly bigger hole than F2 states. R9 ('avoid a new table', 04:27) genuinely has no test because it is a non-testable design constraint validated only by the Database Readiness Gates (05:70-77) — F2's 'R9 has no test' is literally true but is a weaker point than the R8 gap. Net: confirmed, high severity stands; the matrix is absent and would expose real holes.

### [HIGH] B5-F3 — No test inventory mapping the 3 existing tests that MUST change to their new assertions  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md 'Add Row Flow'; 04-implementation-plan.md U2
- **Plan claim:** 01 says existing tests encode current behavior at estimate-sheet-library-insert.test.tsx:247, estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:220 and :230. U2 lists those two test files under Files. 05 lists 5 bun test commands.
- **Ground truth:** Verified: estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:220 asserts 'adds a blank estimate row when the area drop zone is keyboard-activated' and finds label 'Drag or add a component here empty estimate component slot' (the BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL at activity-composer.ts:28). :230 asserts 'keeps persisted blank estimate rows as placeholder slots'. The library-insert assertion the plan cites at :247 is actually the tail of a prior test; the 'sends an unlinked manual row template' test starts at :251. These three existing tests will BREAK or need rewriting once Add row creates an express editor instead of the blank placeholder slot, but the pack never enumerates which assertions flip from 'blank placeholder present' to 'express editor present'.
- **Detail:** For a day-0 mission-critical change, the single highest-risk activity is rewriting tests that currently lock in the OLD behavior. The plan names the files but not the specific assertions that must invert, nor whether the blank-placeholder tests should be kept (for legacy persisted rows, which the plan says must still render) or replaced. Without a test inventory an implementer may delete legacy-blank coverage that the plan explicitly requires to survive (RSK: 'Legacy blank rows must remain readable').
- **Evidence:** tests/vitest/estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:220, tests/vitest/estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:230, tests/vitest/estimate-sheet-library-insert.test.tsx:251, src/lib/activity/activity-composer.ts:28
- **Fix:** Author a TEST PLAN / TEST INVENTORY doc (see missingDocuments). For each of the 3 existing tests: state keep-as-is vs modify vs split, and exactly which assertion changes. Add a dedicated 'legacy persisted blank row still renders as placeholder' test that must stay green to satisfy the legacy-readability requirement, separate from the new express-editor tests.
- **Adversarial verify:** `corrected` (sev→high) — The CORE gap F3 names is confirmed: the pack names the affected test files (U2, 04:71) but never enumerates which specific assertions must invert from 'blank placeholder present' to 'express editor present', nor whether the legacy persisted-blank tests are kept or replaced — a real, high-risk omission for a day-0 change that rewrites the Add row path while RSK ('Legacy blank rows must remain readable', 05:85) requires legacy blanks to survive. However, F3's EVIDENCE contains two factual errors that must be corrected: (1) F3 quotes the keyboard-activation test as finding label 'Drag or add a component here empty estimate component slot' and attributes that string to BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL. WRONG on both counts. The keyboard-activation test (it() at estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:218, assertion at :227) actually finds 'Drag or add a component here n' (the cell aria-label is `${BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL} n` from cells.tsx). The 'empty estimate component slot' label exists in a DIFFERENT test — the persisted-blank test 'keeps persisted blank estimate rows...' (it() at :230, assertion at :266). And the constant BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL (activity-composer.ts:28) equals only 'Drag or add a component here' — the suffixes ' n' / 'empty estimate component slot' are appended in the cell/render layer, not in the constant. (2) F3 claims 'the library-insert assertion the plan cites at :247 is actually the tail of a prior test; the test starts at :251.' WRONG: library-insert.test.tsx:247 is the it('sends an unlinked manual row template when the area drop zone is clicked') declaration itself; :251 is the user.click INSIDE that test; the prior test ('sends a UUID row id...') ends at :245. So 01-audit's citation of :247 correctly points at the start (it() line) of the cited test, not a stray tail. F3's behavioral descriptions are otherwise right: :220's test (it@218) asserts the blank slot via keyboard activation, :230 asserts persisted blank rows render as placeholder slots, and all three will break/need rewrite once Add row produces an express editor. Net: the gap is genuine and high-severity, but two evidentiary specifics in F3 are inaccurate and corrected above.

### [MEDIUM] B5-F4 — No edge-case / failure-mode catalog; several named risks lack a concrete handling spec  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md 'Release Risks'; 06-review-and-risk-register.md Risks
- **Plan claim:** 05 'Release Risks' and 06 RSK1-RSK9 enumerate risks (insert race, stale revision, projection leak, promotion duplicate, virtualization mismeasure). UX 03 mentions Retry/Delete draft recovery.
- **Ground truth:** Risks are listed but not turned into deterministic handling rules. Concrete failure modes that have no spec: (a) concurrent edit of the same express row from two tabs — the update path uses expectedRowRevision (use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:54, actions.ts UpdateEstimateRowItemInput:91) and rolls back on stale revision, but the plan never says what the user sees or how the buffered-commit queue behaves on a 409; (b) very large description — material column is minmax(160px,1fr) (row-table.tsx:88 per 01) and virtualization assumes stable height, no max-length or truncation threshold is given; (c) image URL that 404s — UX says 'fallback behavior' but no spec; (d) promotion partial failure (library item created, row relink fails) — RSK8 says 'idempotency guard' but the catalog of what state the row is left in is absent; (e) duplicate detach race on the icon-bug rows.
- **Detail:** Risks-as-a-table is not the same as a failure-mode catalog with required behavior. For a mission-critical editor the implementer needs, per failure: trigger, detection, user-visible result, data-integrity invariant, and test. The insert-race and promotion-partial-failure cases in particular are correctness-critical and currently rely on prose like 'keep visible draft recovery'.
- **Evidence:** src/hooks/use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:54, src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:91, /Users/philiplewis/Documents/archestimator/.ai/03_plans/2026-06-15-Express-Components/03-ux-interaction-design.md
- **Fix:** Author an EDGE-CASE & FAILURE-MODE catalog (see missingDocuments) enumerating insert race, stale-revision concurrent edit, large description, image error, promotion partial failure, duplicate detach, offline/timeout — each with trigger/detection/UX/invariant/test.

For a day-0 change to the core editor on a live project (ebxxqidacheyapmmzavr), a rollout/rollback plan is mandatory.

- **Fix:** Author a ROLLOUT & SEQUENCING doc (see missingDocuments): unit dependency graph, shippable slices, feature-flag/kill-switch for Add-row template, the optional promotion-RPC migration runbook with up/down/verify, and a rollback procedure.

### [MEDIUM] B5-F8 — Security/threat boundary for the project-folder projection is named but not modeled at plan level  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 02 'Project Folder In Components Tab'; 04 U6; 06 RSK5
- **Plan claim:** 02 and U6/KTD6/RSK5 require an explicit projectId and 'a dedicated project-scoped action that checks project/estimate access with the same boundary as estimate reads; do not reuse a workspace-library membership query.'
- **Ground truth:** The plan correctly identifies the trust boundary and even cites the right RLS predicates (can_access_estimate for reads, verified in 01:100 and live evidence). But there is no threat model: no enumeration of the leak vectors (workspace-member-but-not-estimate-viewer seeing project rows; projectId tampering in query/context; the projection action trusting a client-supplied projectId without re-deriving estimate access). The Components route currently loads getCurrentWorkspaceComponentRowsLibrary (LibraryComponentsRoute.tsx:9 per 01) on workspace membership only, so a projection bolted onto that surface is a real escalation path.
- **Detail:** For mission-critical day-0 work touching authz, 'check project/estimate access' as a sentence is insufficient. The implementer needs the exact predicate to call, what identifier is trusted (projectId from where, re-validated how), and the negative tests. Q20 asserts the workspace-only route does not fetch project rows, but there is no Q for the cross-tenant leak (workspace member without estimate access).
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/library/LibraryComponentsRoute.tsx:9, /Users/philiplewis/Documents/archestimator/.ai/03_plans/2026-06-15-Express-Components/02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md, /Users/philiplewis/Documents/archestimator/.ai/03_plans/2026-06-15-Express-Components/06-review-and-risk-register.md
- **Fix:** Author a plan-level THREAT MODEL section/doc (see missingDocuments) for the projection: trust boundary diagram, projectId provenance and re-validation, the exact access predicate (can_access_estimate), and negative authz tests (workspace member without estimate access must see no project rows).

- **08-traceability-matrix.md** — Prove every requirement is covered by a unit, an acceptance example, and a QA scenario, and expose holes before implementation.

    - Add missing coverage for R8 regression (move/clear/delete/select/keyboard/virtualization unaffected), dashboard aggregation AE (U8), and a no-new-table assertion for R9
    - Bidirectional check: every AE and every Q must map back to at least one R
    - Sign-off checklist: no requirement left without a test
- **09-test-plan-and-inventory.md** — Map each existing test that must change to its new assertions and enumerate every new test, so test rewrites do not delete required legacy coverage.
    - Frontmatter
    - Existing tests impacted: estimate-sheet-library-insert.test.tsx (~:251), estimate-sheet-cost-inputs.test.tsx:220 and :230 — for each: keep/modify/split and the exact assertion that flips from blank-placeholder to express-editor
    - Legacy-preservation tests: explicit 'persisted blank row still renders as placeholder' test that must stay green (RSK legacy readability)
    - New unit tests per U1-U8 with file path and key assertions
    - Integration/action tests: JSON-merge preservation (Q19), promotion idempotency/retry (Q21), projection authz negative test
    - Browser-smoke checklist (already in 05) cross-referenced
    - Test command sequence and gating order (focused -> typecheck -> lint -> validate)
- **10-edge-cases-and-failure-modes.md** — Convert the risk table into deterministic failure-mode handling with required behavior and tests for a mission-critical editor.
    - Frontmatter
    - Per-failure template: trigger / detection / user-visible result / data-integrity invariant / test

    - State copy: Creating/dirty/saving/saved/failed, Retry/Delete draft buttons, promotion success/failure toasts
