## Lane B6-coherence-consistency — readiness 78/100
**Summary:** The Express Components pack is unusually coherent for a day-0 plan: the core link_state vocabulary is grounded in the real enum (`unlinked`, `linked-clean`, `linked-overridden`, `detached`, `source-changed` — confirmed at supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:10), and doc 02's claim that express rows use `link_state="unlinked"` is correct, not an implementer trap. RSK1-RSK9 map cleanly onto the doc-06 review findings, KTDs, and units, and the seven P1 resolutions each name a concrete owning unit. The main coherence defects are terminology drift in cost-field naming (the pack freely mixes `unit_cost`/`itemCost`/`unitCost` and `adjustment`/`contingencyPercentage` across docs without a glossary), and a missing explicit traceability spine: the QA matrix Q1-Q21 references no R or U IDs, so R-to-U-to-AE-to-Q linkage must be reconstructed by the reader and several requirements (R8, R9, R10) have no clearly-owned Q row. The deepest substantive coherence gap is that the blank-vs-express distinction the whole pack rests on collides with the real `isBlankEstimateRowItem` heuristic (activity-history.tsx:120-127), which classifies any source="project" + default-material + zero-cost + Unassigned row as blank regardless of metadata — a freshly-created, unnamed express row would match it, yet the pack's express detection is specified as metadata-based and never reconciles the two. Overall the pack is internally consistent enough to implement, but needs a one-line canonical glossary, an explicit traceability table, and a resolution of the blank/express classification overlap.

**Dimension scores:** Terminology consistency 72; Traceability (R->U->AE->Q) 68; Internal contradictions 82; Risk/finding coherence (RSK<->doc06) 90; Stop-condition satisfaction 74

**Strengths:**
- link_state vocabulary is correct and consistent: doc 02 line 36's `link_state="unlinked"`, doc 02 line 62's `linked-clean`, line 74's `detached`, and line 99's `linked-overridden` all match the real enum at supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:10 (`'unlinked','linked-clean','linked-overridden','detached','source-changed'`). No phantom enum values like "linked" or "source_changed" appear as data values.
- RSK1-RSK9 (doc 06 lines 17-25) line up tightly with the doc 06 review-findings table (lines 33-40) and with the owning units in doc 04: RSK2->U2/U4, RSK3->U3, RSK4->U5, RSK5/RSK6->U6, RSK8->U7, RSK9->U2/U8. The risk register is not orphaned from the plan.
- The promotion-default story is internally consistent across docs: doc 02 line 62, KTD4 (doc 04 line 36), R11 (line 29), and U7 (line 110) all agree promotion relinks the row to `link_state="linked-clean"` and that detaching again is a subsequent optional user action — there is no contradiction between 'relink to linked-clean' and 'users can detach again'.
- doc 03's express-badge behavior (line 73: express row shows Express/Project badge + promote, not make-project-specific) agrees with R4/R6 and U5/U7, and is consistent with the real gate: `canMakeProjectSpecific` (cells.tsx:79-81) already returns false for blank rows, so express rows naturally fall outside it once they are non-blank.
- The `row_blank_added` mislabel risk (doc 02 line 52, RSK9, doc 06 line 36) is a real, correctly-identified trap: the insert RPC branches purely on `source_library_item_id is null` (supabase/migrations/20260524033151_estimate_sheet_version_control.sql:384), and express rows are source-less, so they would be mislabeled. The metadata needed to narrow the branch is written before the event row (migration line 359), so the proposed fix is feasible.

**Findings:**

### [HIGH] B6-F1 — QA matrix Q1-Q21 carries no R/U traceability IDs; the R->U->AE->Q spine must be reconstructed by the reader  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md:15-37 (scenario matrix); 04-implementation-plan.md:18-29 (R1-R11), 60-118 (U1-U8), 120-128 (AE1-AE6)
- **Plan claim:** INDEX 'Stop Condition' (INDEX.md:30) says an implementer should know exactly which surfaces to change and have regression targets; doc 04 lists R1-R11, U1-U8, AE1-AE6; doc 05 lists Q1-Q21.
- **Ground truth:** The QA matrix rows Q1-Q21 reference no requirement or unit IDs at all (verified: rg for R/U/AE tokens in 05 returns only the Q-row text itself). The mapping is only implicit. Reconstructing it surfaces under-covered requirements: R8 (drag/drop/move/clear/delete/selection/keyboard/virtualization stays functional) has no dedicated Q row — only a browser-smoke bullet (05:68); R9 (avoid new table) and R10 (pending-insert lifecycle) have no own Q (R10 is partially covered by Q17/Q18); and AE2 (edit+blur recalculates subtotal/total) is split across Q2/Q5 with no single owning Q.
- **Detail:** For a mission-critical day-0 handoff the absence of an explicit requirement->unit->acceptance->QA table is a coherence/traceability defect: a follow-on implementer cannot confirm every requirement has a test owner without re-deriving the graph, and the re-derivation exposes that R8/R9/R10 lack first-class verification rows.
- **Evidence:** 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md:15-37, 04-implementation-plan.md:18-29, 04-implementation-plan.md:26
- **Fix:** Add a single traceability table to doc 04 or doc 05 with columns R | U(s) | AE(s) | Q(s). Add an explicit Q row for R8 regression (drag/replace, move, clear, delete, selection, keyboard nav, virtualization on express rows) and for R9 (assert no migration is required by default / migration-gated). Annotate each Q row with its owning R and U.
- **Adversarial verify:** `confirmed` (sev→high) — CONFIRMED. The QA matrix carries no R/U/AE traceability tokens: `rg '\bR[0-9]+\b|\bU[0-9]+\b|\bAE[0-9]+\b' 05-readiness-qa-matrix.md` returns NO MATCHES. Q1-Q21 (05:17-37) reference scenarios and evidence-required prose only; the R->U->AE->Q spine is implicit and must be re-derived by the reader. Re-deriving it confirms the under-coverage the finding names: (a) R8 — 'Existing drag/drop replace, move, clear, delete, row selection, keyboard navigation, and row virtualization remain functional' (04:26) — has NO dedicated Q row. It appears only as two Browser Smoke bullets (05:67 keyboard path, 05:68 drag/drop path) and one Release Risk (05:81 virtualization). The 'Delete' token in Q18 (05:34) is the insert-failure Retry/Delete recovery UI, NOT a regression test of row deletion. (b) R9 ('avoid a new table', 04:27) has no Q row; it surfaces only as Database Readiness Gates (05:73). Note: R9 is largely a design/architecture constraint rather than a behaviorally testable requirement, which mildly softens its 'missing test owner' framing, but it still illustrates the absent traceability table. (c) R10 (pending-insert lifecycle, 04:28) is partially owned by Q17/Q18 (05:33-34) — fair characterization. (d) AE2 (edit+blur recalculates subtotal/total, 04:123) is split across Q2 (name edit, commit-on-blur, 05:18) and Q5 (unit cost recalculates subtotal/total, 05:21) with no single owning Q. All cited evidence ranges verified: 05:15-37 is the matrix table; 04:18-29 spans R1-R11 (R1 actually begins at 04:19, off-by-one at the top boundary, immaterial). INDEX Stop Condition (INDEX.md:30) does promise 'knows exactly which repo surfaces to change, has regression targets for the project-specific icon bug' — note the regression-target promise is explicitly scoped to the icon bug, not all requirements, which makes the missing whole-requirement traceability table an even clearer gap relative to the pack's own stated bar. High severity is justified for a mission-critical day-0 handoff.

### [HIGH] B6-F2 — Blank-vs-express classification is metadata-based in the plan but material/cost-based in the real `isBlankEstimateRowItem`; an unnamed express row collides with the blank heuristic  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md:63-66 (U1), 68-75 (U2); 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:95-98 (inclusion rules)
- **Plan claim:** doc 04 U1 (line 65) says 'add express helpers based on `metadata.expressComponent` or tags'; doc 02 lines 96-98 say to include express rows and exclude rows that `isBlankEstimateRowItem` still classifies as the Add row placeholder; U2 (line 72) says preserve compatibility for old persisted placeholders.
- **Ground truth:** `isBlankEstimateRowItem` (src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:120-127) returns true if the row is tagged `manual-row-placeholder` OR matches a tag-independent heuristic: `source === 'project'` AND `material === 'Drag or add a component here'` AND `itemCost === 0` AND `category === 'Unassigned'` AND no `imageUrl`. A just-created express row (source 'project', default material, zero cost, Unassigned, no image, and NOT carrying the legacy tag) still matches lines 122-127 until the user fills a field. The current blank check never consults `metadata`.
- **Detail:** The pack's express-detection design (metadata-based) and the real blank-detection logic (material/cost/category heuristic) are not reconciled anywhere in the pack. This threatens internal coherence of the central blank-vs-express distinction that runs through doc 01 (line 30), doc 02 (lines 79-80, 95-98), U1, U2, Q1, and Q12: an unnamed express row could be wrongly treated as blank (mislabeled history, excluded from the Project folder) or, if the heuristic is loosened, legacy blanks could leak into the Project folder.
- **Evidence:** src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/activity-history.tsx:120, 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:95, 04-implementation-plan.md:65
- **Fix:** Add an explicit decision in U1/U2 stating that the express template must carry a positive express marker (e.g. `metadata.expressComponent.kind='express'`) AND that `isBlankEstimateRowItem` (or a new `isExpressRowItem`) must be updated so the material/cost/category heuristic yields to the express marker. Add a QA row asserting an unnamed/zero-cost express row is NOT classified blank and DOES appear in the Project folder.
- **Adversarial verify:** `overstated` (sev→medium) — OVERSTATED. The code facts in the finding are accurate, but the central 'active collision' claim ignores a load-bearing line in the plan and does not survive scrutiny. CONFIRMED facts: isBlankEstimateRowItem (activity-history.tsx:120-134) returns true when source==='project' AND material===BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL AND (tagged OR the isPersistedPlaceholder heuristic at lines 122-127: source==='project' && material===BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL && itemCost===0 && category==='Unassigned' && !imageUrl). BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL === 'Drag or add a component here' (activity-composer.ts:28), so the finding's literal-string description is correct. The blank check never consults metadata — CONFIRMED (no metadata reference inside lines 120-134). The current blank template (EstimateSheetContent.tsx:220-241) sets material: BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL and tags: [BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_TAG]. WHERE THE FINDING OVERSTATES: it claims 'a just-created express row (... default material ...) still matches lines 122-127 until the user fills a field.' That is only true if the express template reuses BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL as its material. The plan explicitly mandates the OPPOSITE — U2 Tests (04:75): 'Add row sends source: "project", non-placeholder material default, express metadata...'. Because material===BLANK_ESTIMATE_ROW_COMPONENT_LABEL is a CONJUNCTIVE gate in BOTH the outer condition (line 131) and the isPersistedPlaceholder branch (line 124), a non-placeholder material default makes isBlankEstimateRowItem return false outright — no collision, no reliance on metadata. The finding never engages with 04:75. THE VALID RESIDUAL (lower severity): the pack does not state anywhere that the express material default is the load-bearing safeguard against the legacy blank heuristic, and it does not reconcile the metadata-based express detection (U1 04:65 'express helpers based on metadata.expressComponent or tags'; 02:95) against the material-based blank heuristic in one place; 02:98 and 04:65 both lean on isBlankEstimateRowItem as the exclusion gate without flagging that its material/cost/category logic is independent of metadata. So a real coherence/under-specification gap exists, but it is a 'this safeguard is implicit and unstated' gap, not the 'unnamed express row collides with the blank heuristic' active defect the finding describes. Corrected to medium.

### [MEDIUM] B6-F3 — Cost-field terminology drifts across docs: `unit_cost` vs `itemCost` vs `unitCost` for the same concept, with no glossary  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md:54; 04-implementation-plan.md:81; 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:37
- **Plan claim:** doc 01 line 54 lists needed fields as `unit_cost`, `misc_cost` (snake_case); doc 04 U3 line 81 lists patch fields as `itemCost`, `miscCost` (camelCase domain); doc 02 line 37 lists `unit_cost`, `misc_cost`; doc 05 references 'unit cost'.
- **Ground truth:** Three distinct names for unit cost coexist in the real code and all are 'correct' at different layers: DB column `unit_cost` (supabase/migrations/.../20260524033151:332), raw snapshot/library field `unitCost` (src/lib/estimates/actions.ts:328,351,2470), and domain row field `itemCost` (src/lib/estimates/estimate-row-tree-patch.ts:5; src/hooks/use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:80,232; src/components/workshop/EstimateSheetContent.tsx:228). The canonical domain-patch field an implementer must add in U3 is `itemCost`, which doc 04:81 uses correctly, but doc 01:54 and doc 02:37 use the DB/snapshot names without saying which layer.
- **Detail:** This is layer-ambiguity rather than a hard error, but it is exactly the terminology drift the coherence lane targets: an implementer reading doc 01/02 might add a `unit_cost` field to `EstimateRowItemPatch` (which actually uses `itemCost`) and break the typed patch. The pack never states the three-name mapping.
- **Evidence:** 01-current-state-audit.md:54, 04-implementation-plan.md:81, src/lib/estimates/estimate-row-tree-patch.ts:5
- **Fix:** Add a one-paragraph field glossary to doc 04 (or INDEX): DB column `unit_cost`/`misc_cost`/`contingency_percentage` <-> JSON payload key `unitCost`/`miscCost`/`linkState` <-> domain field `itemCost`/`miscCost`/`contingencyPercentage`. State that U3 patch fields are the domain names (`itemCost`, `miscCost`).

### [LOW] B6-F4 — 'adjustment' (doc 01) vs 'contingencyPercentage' (doc 01/02/04) used for the same field within the same pack  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 01-current-state-audit.md:35 vs 01-current-state-audit.md:47
- **Plan claim:** doc 01 line 35 says the row table 'edit[s] quantity, adjustment, and misc cost only'; doc 01 line 47 says the update path allows 'quantity, contingencyPercentage, and miscCost'.
- **Ground truth:** The data field is `contingencyPercentage` (src/hooks/use-estimate-row-mutations.ts:52; src/lib/estimates/estimate-row-tree-patch.ts:6). 'Adjustment' is only the UI surface term: the row-table control uses `CostAdjustmentMode` and aria-labels '...adjustment type' / '...adjustment percentage' (src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/row-table.tsx:13,275,314). So both names are real but at different layers, and doc 01 uses both within four lines without noting they are the same field.
- **Detail:** Minor coherence wrinkle. The two sentences in doc 01 describe the same editable field with two names ('adjustment' and 'contingencyPercentage'), which could read as two separate fields to an implementer skimming the audit.
- **Evidence:** 01-current-state-audit.md:35, 01-current-state-audit.md:47, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/row-table.tsx:314
- **Fix:** In doc 01:35 write 'quantity, adjustment (contingencyPercentage), and misc cost' to bind the UI term to the data field, and fold this into the F3 glossary.

### [LOW] B6-F5 — Linked-overridden rows: Cube action shown (doc 03) but excluded from Project folder (doc 02/03/04) — defensible but the rationale split is not stated together  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 03-ux-interaction-design.md:71 vs 03-ux-interaction-design.md:57; 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:99; 04-implementation-plan.md:103
- **Plan claim:** doc 03 line 71 'Linked overridden row still shows Cube action'; doc 03 line 57 and doc 02 line 99 and U6 (doc 04:103) exclude `linked-overridden` rows from the Project folder projection.
- **Ground truth:** The two behaviors are consistent with the real gate, not contradictory: `canMakeProjectSpecific` returns true for `linked-overridden` because `linkState?.startsWith('linked')` is true (src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/cells.tsx:81), so showing the Cube action is correct; and excluding such rows from the project-local projection is a deliberate scoping choice (they remain customized library instances). The two facts are correct but appear in different sections without a sentence reconciling 'eligible for make-project-specific' vs 'not a project-local component'.
- **Detail:** A reviewer could misread this as a contradiction (a row that can be made project-specific but is excluded from the Project folder). It is actually coherent: make-project-specific eligibility (link still present) and project-local membership (link severed) are different predicates.
- **Evidence:** 03-ux-interaction-design.md:71, 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:99, src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/cells.tsx:81
- **Fix:** Add one sentence in U6 or doc 03 noting that linked-overridden rows are deliberately make-project-specific-eligible (still linked) yet excluded from the Project folder until detached, so the dual treatment is obviously intentional.

### [MEDIUM] B6-F6 — 'unlinked' is treated as the express marker in doc 02 but inclusion rules also include 'unlinked but not blank' rows — express vs legacy-unlinked rows are not disambiguated by link_state alone  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:36,79,95-97
- **Plan claim:** doc 02 line 36 sets express `link_state='unlinked'`; doc 02 line 79 says 'Express row: authored locally, never linked'; doc 02 line 97 includes in the Project folder 'rows that are unlinked but no longer blank placeholders' in ADDITION to express-metadata rows (line 95).
- **Ground truth:** `unlinked` is the DB default link_state for ANY source-less row (migration 20260524033151:358; seed rows at 20260518110000_attach_philip_seed_data.sql:168-180 are plain `unlinked` manual rows, not express). So `link_state='unlinked'` cannot by itself mean 'express' — many existing manual rows are unlinked. Doc 02 simultaneously (a) uses metadata.expressComponent as the express marker and (b) includes generic 'unlinked but not blank' rows in the projection, which would sweep in pre-existing plain manual rows that were never authored as express.
- **Detail:** This is an internal coherence tension in the inclusion rules: line 95 keys on express metadata, but line 97 broadens to all non-blank unlinked rows, which conflicts with the doc's own definition (line 79) that express rows are specifically metadata-tagged. The two inclusion bullets can disagree on whether a legacy unlinked manual row belongs in the Project folder.
- **Evidence:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:95, 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:97, supabase/migrations/20260518110000_attach_philip_seed_data.sql:168
- **Fix:** Decide and state explicitly whether the Project folder includes (a) only metadata-tagged express rows + detached rows, or (b) all non-blank project rows. If (a), drop the line 97 'unlinked but not blank' bullet or qualify it to express-metadata rows; if (b), update the line 79 definition so express is not the sole inclusion key. Make U6's inclusion predicate match.

### [MEDIUM] B6-F7 — Stop Condition promises 'regression targets for the project-specific icon bug' but the pack never roots-causes the bug; U5 only lists candidate hypotheses  _(type: gap)_
- **Plan loc:** INDEX.md:30; 01-current-state-audit.md:66-73; 04-implementation-plan.md:92-97
- **Plan claim:** INDEX Stop Condition (INDEX.md:30) says the plan is ready when the implementer 'has regression targets for the project-specific icon bug'. Doc 01 lines 66-72 list four candidate failure modes; U5 (doc 04:92-97) says to 'verify cloneLibraryItemForEstimate and server reconciliation preserve sourceLibraryItemId and linked linkState'.
- **Ground truth:** The gate condition itself is real and correctly cited: `canMakeProjectSpecific` requires `sourceLibraryItemId` OR a linked/source-changed link_state (src/components/workshop/estimate-sheet/rows/cells.tsx:79-81), so a library insert that lands without those fields would hide the action — consistent with the plan's hypotheses. But the pack provides regression TARGETS (Q8/Q9, AE3/AE4) without an identified ROOT CAUSE: doc 01:66-72 offers four mutually-exclusive hypotheses and U5 does not commit to one.
- **Detail:** Internally, the Stop Condition claims regression targets exist (true: Q8/Q9, AE3/AE4) but the pack's own framing leaves the actual defect undiagnosed, so the regression tests assert the desired end-state without proving the fix addresses the real cause. This is a coherence gap between INDEX's confidence and U5's open-ended investigation, not a factual error.
- **Evidence:** INDEX.md:30, 01-current-state-audit.md:66, 04-implementation-plan.md:92
- **Fix:** Either (a) downgrade the Stop Condition wording to 'has regression targets and a diagnosis task for the icon bug', or (b) add a short investigation unit before U5 that pins the actual root cause (instrument `cloneLibraryItemForEstimate` and the insert payload to confirm whether `sourceLibraryItemId`/`linkState` survive for the first vs second instance) so the regression test is tied to a known cause.

### [LOW] B6-F8 — R7/AE5 say the Project folder shows 'unique project-specific rows' but doc 02/03/U6 inclusion rules never define the uniqueness/dedupe predicate  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** 04-implementation-plan.md:25 (R7) vs 04-implementation-plan.md:99-104 (U6), 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:95-99
- **Plan claim:** R7 (doc 04:25) 'containing express rows and unique project-specific rows'; AE5 (doc 04:127) says express and detached rows appear under Project; doc 02:95-99 and U6 (doc 04:103) give inclusion rules but no dedupe rule.
- **Ground truth:** The TemplatesTree project-source path the pack cites does perform dedupe of checked project-source targets (src/components/workshop/component-library/tree/TemplatesTree.tsx:479 'dedupes checked project-source targets', cited in doc 01:90), so 'unique' is a meaningful real concern, but the Express-Components inclusion rules (doc 02:95-99, U6) list include/exclude predicates and never say what 'unique' means for express/detached rows (per row? per material? per source snapshot?).
- **Detail:** R7 introduces a 'unique' qualifier that no downstream unit, inclusion rule, or QA row operationalizes (Q10/Q11/Q12 test presence/exclusion, not uniqueness). The requirement and its implementation spec drift on whether dedupe is required.
- **Evidence:** 04-implementation-plan.md:25, 04-implementation-plan.md:99, src/components/workshop/component-library/tree/TemplatesTree.tsx:479
- **Fix:** Either drop 'unique' from R7 (each estimate row is its own node) or define the dedupe key in U6 and add a QA row asserting it. Align AE5 and Q10/Q11 with the chosen semantics.

### [INFO] B6-F9 — Strength: link_state enum vocabulary is fully consistent across the pack and matches the real enum (no phantom values)  _(type: strength)_
- **Plan loc:** 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:36,62,74,99; 03-ux-interaction-design.md:57,70,72; 01-current-state-audit.md:62
- **Plan claim:** Across docs the link_state values used are `unlinked` (02:36), `linked-clean` (02:62, KTD4), `detached` (02:74, 03:72), `linked-overridden` (02:99, 03:57), `source-changed` (01:62, 03:70).
- **Ground truth:** Real enum `public.estimate_row_link_state as enum ('unlinked','linked-clean','linked-overridden','detached','source-changed')` (supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:10). Every link_state token used as a data value in the pack matches exactly; no incorrect variant ('linked', 'source_changed', 'linked_clean') is used as a data value. The lane's hypothesized 'unlinked vs detached' trap does not materialize — both are real and used correctly.
- **Detail:** This removes the single most dangerous potential implementer trap the lane was asked to hunt: a wrong enum string. The pack is clean on this axis.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260518090000_archestimator_core_schema.sql:10, 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:36, 03-ux-interaction-design.md:70
- **Fix:** No change required. Optionally cite schema line 10 in doc 02 to make the enum provenance explicit for the implementer.

### [LOW] B6-F10 — INDEX Primary Decision references promotion via 'cost_library_item_price_options RPC surface' while U7/doc 02 cite a create action, not a price-option RPC by name — minor surface-naming drift  _(type: inconsistency)_
- **Plan loc:** INDEX.md:13; 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:58-62; 04-implementation-plan.md:36 (KTD4),53,106-111

- **Ground truth:** Doc 02:58 cites `src/lib/component-library/actions.ts:431` as the create entry point (not verified line-exact in this lane — Lane B-data owns that), and the mermaid node `create_cost_library_component` (doc 04:53) is a plan-proposed RPC name, not asserted to exist. INDEX's phrase 'cost_library_item_price_options RPC surface' is looser than the unit-level description and could read as naming a specific existing RPC.
- **Detail:** Low-impact naming drift between the INDEX summary ('RPC surface') and the concrete unit (a create action + price-option writes + a proposed wrapper RPC). Not a contradiction, but the three docs use three different names for the promotion mechanism.
- **Evidence:** INDEX.md:13, 02-data-model-and-supabase-research.md:58, 04-implementation-plan.md:53
- **Fix:** Normalize promotion terminology: in INDEX say 'existing component-library create action plus price-option writes (see U7)', and mark `create_cost_library_component` in the mermaid as a proposed wrapper, so the same mechanism is named consistently across INDEX, doc 02, and U7.

---

## Completeness Critic
The 13-lane audit is deep and citation-rigorous on the STATIC, single-user, single-surface dimensions: insert path (A1), virtualization (A2), update contract / JSON-replace (A3), the icon bug (A4), Components projection (A5), data model / RPC SQL (A6), promotion atomicity (A7), strategy (B1), accessibility (B2), blast radius (B3), downstream surfaces (B4), missing docs (B5), and coherence (B6). Those lanes converge on a credible aggregate readiness in the high-50s/low-60s and surface the genuinely hard issues (fixed 96px virtualizer overlap, JSON wholesale-replace, two-transaction promotion, undiagnosed U5, blank-vs-express classification collision). HOWEVER, the audit as a whole has a consistent blind spot: it audited the feature as a SINGLE-USER, DESKTOP, NEVER-ROLLED-BACK feature. The single largest under-covered dimension is REALTIME / MULTI-CLIENT CONCURRENCY: I verified the estimate sheet has a live, cell-anchored collaboration subsystem (src/lib/collaboration/*, src/hooks/use-estimate-cursor-broadcast.ts, and EstimateSheetContent.tsx resolveCursorTarget at ~line 771 anchoring remote cursors to data-estimate-row-id / data-estimate-cell-index), yet no lane and no plan doc analyzes two users authoring/editing express rows at once. The whole U2/U4 'buffer commits until persisted row revision' contract is designed against a single client; under two clients, optimistic client-generated row ids, the expected_row_revision check (enforced in src/lib/estimates/actions.ts), and a peer seeing an in-flight draft row are all unverified. Secondary systemic gaps the audit barely touches: mobile/touch (zero coverage, and it strictly worsens both the 96px-overlap and arrow-vs-caret problems), permission/role gating (the canEditEstimate gate exists in EstimateSheetContent but no lane verified read-only collaborators are blocked, nor the estimate-edit-vs-workspace-editor privilege boundary that promotion crosses), operational rollback / kill-switch (no feature flag exists and no lane checked it), analytics/telemetry corruption from the row_blank_added mislabel, i18n of new microcopy, and the forward-only-vs-existing-data question for the row_blank_added reclassification. Finally, several lanes independently flagged that U5's root cause is never reproduced — but no lane was tasked to actually reproduce it, so the bug fix remains genuinely unverified, not merely 'under-specified.' These are not nitpicks: the concurrency, permission, and rollback gaps are exactly the failure modes that turn a 'mission-critical day-0' build into an incident.

**Coverage gaps the audit itself under-covered:**

- **Realtime / multi-client concurrency on express rows (collaborative editing)** — Ground-truth verified: the estimate sheet runs a live collaboration subsystem with cell-level cursor anchoring (src/lib/collaboration/*, src/hooks/use-estimate-cursor-broadcast.ts; EstimateSheetContent.tsx resolveCursorTarget ~line 771 anchors remote cursors to data-estimate-row-id + data-estimate-cell-index, and EstimateSheetCursorLayer broadcasts pointer/cell context). No lane and no plan doc analyzes two users in the same estimate concurrently. The entire U2/U4 'buffer commits until persisted row revision' contract is single-client by construction: client-generated optimistic row ids could collide or diverge across clients, a peer has no rendering path for an in-flight draft express row that exists only in another client's local React state (Lane B1 noted insert mutates local state, not the TanStack cache), and the expected_row_revision check (src/lib/estimates/actions.ts) will produce stale-revision rollbacks during concurrent multi-field express edits with no specified UX. This is the most consequential under-covered surface.
    - Suggested: Add a dedicated concurrency unit/QA: (1) define how an in-flight optimistic express row is (or is not) visible to peers and prove the optimistic id cannot collide across clients; (2) specify the end-user recovery UX when an express multi-field edit loses an expected_row_revision race (re-fetch + merge vs discard + warn); (3) add a two-client integration test for simultaneous Add-row and simultaneous field edits on the same row. Reproduce against the real collaboration channel, not a single-client harness.

- **Mobile / responsive / touch behavior** — No lane and no plan doc mentions mobile, touch, viewport, or breakpoints (grep of the pack and src/features/estimates returned nothing relevant). Two already-identified critical issues get strictly worse on touch/narrow screens: Lane A2's fixed-96px-row overlap (an expanding express editor is far more likely to exceed 96px on a narrow column layout) and Lane B2's arrow-nav-vs-text-caret conflict (touch devices have no arrow keys, so the keyboard escape hatch the plan gestures at does not exist; on-screen keyboards also resize the viewport mid-edit). The plan's 'verify with browser smoke' (RSK7) is desktop-implied.
    - Suggested: Add an explicit responsive/touch acceptance row: define express-editor behavior at the narrowest supported viewport, confirm the fixed-height virtualizer constraint holds (or switch the active row to measured height), and specify the touch interaction model for caret vs cell navigation and for set/clear-image without hover affordances.

- **Permission / role gating and the promotion privilege boundary** — EstimateSheetContent gates lifecycle actions behind a canEditEstimate flag (minified to nEstimate in the build), and RLS uses can_edit_estimate for rows but is_workspace_editor for the library tables (confirmed in plan doc 01:100 and Lane A6). No lane verified that (a) read-only / viewer collaborators are blocked from authoring or editing express rows in the UI, or (b) the promotion flow (U7) crosses a privilege boundary: a user with estimate-edit but NOT workspace-editor can author an express row but must be blocked from promoting it to the workspace library. Lane A7 analyzed promotion atomicity but not authorization.
    - Suggested: Add a permission matrix to the plan and QA: estimate-viewer (no author/edit/promote), estimate-editor without workspace-editor (author/edit yes, promote no), and full editor. Verify the UI hides/disables Add-row and Promote for insufficient roles and that the server rejects each, so RLS is not the only line of defense.

- **Operational rollback / kill-switch and in-flight data on revert** — No lane checked deployment rollback, and there is no feature flag for Express Components (grep found none; the collaboration subsystem has feature-flags.ts but express authoring is unguarded). For a 'mission-critical day-0' build this is a major omission: if the build is reverted after users have created express rows, the plan has no answer for how those persisted estimate_row_items (with metadata.expressComponent and non-blank material on link_state='unlinked') render under the OLD code, nor whether the row_blank_added reclassification can be safely rolled back without re-mislabeling.
    - Suggested: Add a rollback runbook (Lane B5 also flagged the missing runbook): decide whether to ship behind a flag, define forward/backward compatibility of express rows against pre-feature code paths, and confirm the SQL classifier change is reversible without corrupting already-written events.

- **Data migration / behavior of EXISTING blank rows and existing row_blank_added events** — Lanes A1/A6/B3/B6 covered the blank-vs-express CLASSIFICATION conflict for NEW rows, but none verified the EXISTING-data dimension end to end: (1) the plan touches it only lightly (QA Q12 and a one-line 'legacy blank rows must remain readable'), and (2) no lane confirmed that already-persisted row_blank_added events for genuine pre-existing blanks are left untouched (forward-only change) versus retroactively reinterpreted, nor that existing persisted blank placeholder rows still render and still get excluded from the Components Project folder under the new express helpers. The reclassification is a live activity-log semantics change on a table that already has history.
    - Suggested: Add an explicit 'existing data' acceptance: confirm the SQL classifier change is forward-only, add a test that an existing blank row + its existing row_blank_added event are unchanged and still excluded from the Project folder, and verify the new express helpers degrade safely on rows that predate metadata.expressComponent.

- **U5 root cause never actually reproduced (bug fix unverified, not just under-specified)** — Lanes A4 and B1 independently concluded U5 fixes a bug whose stated root causes are 'unverified speculation' and 'partly contradicted by the code' (the optimistic clone preserves link fields; real DB-backed components already carry sourceLibraryItemId). But no lane was tasked to REPRODUCE the 'only second instance shows the action' symptom, so the audit collectively asserts the cause is wrong without establishing the right one. A mission-critical fix is being shipped against an unreproduced bug. Lane A4's most concrete lead — that the one reproducible gate failure is the seed/test fixture data, not 'the first instance' — implies the reported symptom may be a fixture artifact rather than a production bug.
    - Suggested: Before implementing U5, require a reproduction: capture the actual failing scenario (production data vs seed fixture), confirm whether the symptom even exists outside test fixtures, and either pin the real mechanism or reframe U5 as a robustness/normalization fix with that justification stated.

- **Analytics / telemetry / activity-dashboard integrity** — No lane checked analytics, and the codebase has telemetry infrastructure (src/lib/collaboration/realtime-telemetry.ts). Two angles are unverified: (1) whether express authoring and promotion should emit any product analytics for a day-0 launch (none specified), and (2) more importantly, whether the CURRENT row_blank_added mislabel (Lanes A1/A6/B4: every source-less row is logged as a blank add, rendered as 'added an empty row slot' and also surfaced in ai/read-context per B4) is already polluting any activity counts or AI context, and whether the fix retroactively corrects or only forward-fixes those metrics.
    - Suggested: Decide and document the analytics surface for express create/edit/promote, and verify whether existing activity/AI surfaces that count or summarize row_blank_added events need the reclassification reflected for correctness.

- **i18n / localization of new microcopy** — No lane checked i18n and the plan doc 03 leaves microcopy undefined (Lane B2 flagged 'no state/copy spec'). New user-facing strings are introduced across insert/save/retry/promotion aria-live announcements and the Components 'Project' folder label. Whether the app has an i18n convention these must follow (vs hard-coded English literals) is unverified, and a day-0 feature that hard-codes strings against a localized app would ship untranslated.
    - Suggested: Verify the repo's existing string/i18n convention and require new express microcopy (announcements, errors, folder label, empty/error states) to follow it; if the app is English-only, state that explicitly so it is a decision, not an omission.

- **Stale-revision / error-budget UX as a first-class user-facing flow** — The plan repeatedly reduces failure to 'stale revision rolls back with failed state' / 'failed state with recovery' (doc 04, doc 05 Q7/Q17/Q18), and Lanes A3/A7 confirmed expected_row_revision is a real conflict surface, but no lane verified the concrete end-user error-budget behavior: what the user sees and can do when a multi-field express edit partially fails, when promotion relink fails after library-create succeeds (Lane A7's orphan), or when repeated retries hit the same revision conflict. These are described as goals, not specified flows.
    - Suggested: Specify the concrete failure-mode catalog Lane B5 also requested, with exact UX for each: revision-conflict on edit, insert failure with draft recovery, and promotion partial-failure (orphaned library item), including whether a retry is automatic or user-initiated and what state persists across a page reload.
